# Intake air filter (iteration 11) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Give each room an intake filter: clogging in the simulator from the fan curve, an optional filter pressure sensor with alarms, a change reminder with a **Filter changed** button, and the intake fan's watts following the filter in the energy totals.

**Architecture:** Pure physics in `device/sim-model.js` (the filter's loading `Lf`, the pressure the sensor reads, the intake share from the fan line) and `device/sim-power.js` (`fanWattFactor`), used by the device app, the farm's simulation runs and the usage estimate. The farm server adds database version 14 (room filter settings, `filter_readings`, `filter_changes`), polls the `dp` sensor, counts a reading only after 60 s of intake (`server/filter.js`) and raises `filterDue`, `filterDirty` and `filterMissing`. The pages add the room dialog's filter section, the room's Intake filter card, the stepper's filter fields and the run page's filter card.

**Tech Stack:** Node 22, `node:sqlite`, plain JS ES modules, no build, `node --test`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md` (v2 `ee4d30a`). Tasks C6, D1 and D2 bring the spec up to date with what was built (the change pressure is what the sensor reads, default 80 Pa; the alarms count across exchanges; a reset keeps the filter's loading; no sensor is `not-wired`).

## How this plan was made and checked

Every block below is the code that was built and run on throwaway branches off `v2` (`ee4d30a`), then generated from those commits: new files whole, changes as unified diffs against the file as the previous task left it.

| Part | Tasks | Tests after | Replay check |
|---|---|---|---|
| A physics, power, device app | A1–A4 | 1110 | applied to `ee4d30a`: byte-identical to the verified branch |
| B farm server | B1–B7 | 1155 | applied after A: `server/` and `tests/` byte-identical |
| C simulation runs, pages, README, spec | C1–C6 | 1180 | applied after A and B: byte-identical |
| D the owner's two decisions (change pressure = sensor reading, 80 Pa; alarms count across exchanges) | D1–D2 | 1183 | applied after C: byte-identical; `npm test` 1183 / 1183 |
| E review fixes (three Opus reviews: server, physics, pages) | E1–E3 | 1197 | applied after D: byte-identical; `npm test` 1197 / 1197 |

Run the parts in order: A, B, C, D, E. B's prerequisite (`fanWattFactor`) is provided by A1. D changes code that A, B and C created; that is intended (the owner decided D after C was built).

## Rules for every task

- Run single tests with `node --disable-warning=ExperimentalWarning --test <files>`, the whole suite with `npm test`.
- Apply a diff block with `git apply <file>` from the repository root (save the block to a file first), or make the same edits by hand.
- Commit messages follow the repo: a plain sentence, no prefix, no trailers. Identity: the repo's configured one.
- Never start, stop or restart the owner's apps (`npm start`, `npm run device`, no `kill`), never open `data/*.db`. A browser check uses a throwaway device and farm app on other ports with temp files.

## Part A: physics, power and the device app

Verified on a branch off `v2` at `ee4d30a`: every block below is the code that passed (`npm test`: 1080 before, 1110 after Part A). Source edits are given as unified diffs: save the block to a file and run `git apply <file>` from the repository root (or make the same edits by hand). New test files are given whole.

Contract names used (see the shared contract): `FILTER_DEFAULTS`, `hasFilter`, `filterOf`, `changeFilter`, state `Lf`, acting `filterPa` / `intakeShare` / `unfilteredM3h` / `filterLoad`, `fanWattFactor`, sensor kind `dp` with value `pa`, sim params `fanMaxPa` / `filterCleanPa` / `filterFinalPa` / `filterLifeDays`, link `filterProbe`, `sims.changeFilter(id)`, `POST /api/sims/:id/change-filter`.

### Task A1: Filter physics and the intake fan's watts

**Files:**
- Modify: `device/sim-model.js` (header, filter block before `clamp`, `initialState`, `full`, `exchangeOf`, new `filterNow` and `intakeW`, `substep`, `nominal`)
- Modify: `device/sim-power.js` (new `fanWattFactor`, intake line of `electricW`)
- Test: `tests/filter-physics.test.js` (new)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** — create `tests/filter-physics.test.js`:

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { FILTER_DEFAULTS, hasFilter, filterOf, changeFilter, initialState, step, wOf, rhOf } from '../device/sim-model.js';
import { fanWattFactor, electricW } from '../device/sim-power.js';

const near = (a, b, tol, msg) => assert.ok(Math.abs(a - b) <= tol, `${msg || ''} ${a} vs ${b} (tol ${tol})`);
const OFF = { intake: false, exhaust: false, circulation: false, humidifier: false, ac: null };
const PAIR = { ...OFF, intake: true, exhaust: true };
// A room with no blocks whose outdoor air equals its own: only the fans and the filter act.
const BASE = { volume: 60, blocks: 0, co2PerBlock: 60, wallUA: 80, sunW: 0, outdoorT: 25, outdoorRH: 70, fanFlow: 600, acKW: 3.5, humidifierGH: 2000 };
const F = { ...BASE, ...FILTER_DEFAULTS };
const at = (Lf, params = F) => ({ ...initialState(params), Lf });
const F_FINAL = 200 / 310; // the intake share at the change pressure with the defaults

function run(state, inputs, params, seconds, dt = 60) {
  let s = state, acting;
  for (let t = 0; t < seconds; t += dt) ({ state: s, acting } = step(s, inputs, params, dt));
  return { state: s, acting };
}

test('the defaults: 200 Pa fan, 40 Pa clean, 150 Pa change, 30 days', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(FILTER_DEFAULTS, { fanMaxPa: 200, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 150, filterLifeDays: 30 });
});

test('hasFilter: the three pressures and a life above zero', () => {
  assert.equal(hasFilter(F), true);
  assert.equal(hasFilter(BASE), false);
  assert.equal(hasFilter({ ...F, filterLifeDays: 0 }), false);
  assert.equal(hasFilter({ ...F, fanMaxPa: null }), false);
  assert.equal(hasFilter({ ...F, filterFinalPa: '150' }), false);
});

test('filterOf: share 1 when clean, 0.65 at the change pressure, less past it; none without a filter', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(filterOf(BASE, 0.7), { share: 1, resistancePa: 0 });
  assert.deepEqual(filterOf(F, 0), { share: 1, resistancePa: 40 });
  const final = filterOf(F, 1);
  near(final.share, F_FINAL, 1e-12);
  assert.equal(final.resistancePa, 150);
  const past = filterOf(F, 2);
  assert.equal(past.resistancePa, 260);
  near(past.share, 200 / 420, 1e-12);
});

test('a new state and a state from before the filter start clean; changeFilter makes it clean', () => {
  assert.equal(initialState(F).Lf, 0);
  const r = step({ T: 25, W: wOf(25, 70), CO2: 500 }, OFF, F, 5);
  assert.equal(r.state.Lf, 0);
  const s = at(0.8);
  assert.deepEqual(changeFilter(s), { ...s, Lf: 0 });
});

test('a clogged filter: the pair moves fanFlow x (1 + f) / 2, the rest of the intake comes through the leaks', () => {
  const r = step(at(1), PAIR, F, 0);
  near(r.acting.exchangeM3h, 600 * (1 + F_FINAL) / 2, 1e-9);
  near(r.acting.intakeShare, F_FINAL, 1e-12);
  near(r.acting.filterPa, 150 * F_FINAL, 1e-9);
  near(r.acting.unfilteredM3h, 600 * (1 - F_FINAL) / 2, 1e-9);
  assert.equal(r.acting.filterLoad, 1);
  // Container Alpha's example in the spec: 150 m³/h, at the change pressure about 124 exchanged, 97 filtered, 27 through the leaks
  const alpha = step(at(1), PAIR, { ...F, fanFlow: 150 }, 0).acting;
  near(alpha.exchangeM3h, 123.4, 0.05);
  near(alpha.exchangeM3h - alpha.unfilteredM3h, 96.8, 0.05);
  near(alpha.unfilteredM3h, 26.6, 0.05);
});

test('one fan alone: the intake moves 30 % x f, the exhaust 30 % as before; no filter pressure without the intake', () => {
  const intake = step(at(1), { ...OFF, intake: true }, F, 0).acting;
  near(intake.exchangeM3h, 0.3 * 600 * F_FINAL, 1e-9);
  near(intake.filterPa, 150 * F_FINAL, 1e-9);
  assert.equal(intake.unfilteredM3h, 0);
  const exhaust = step(at(1), { ...OFF, exhaust: true }, F, 0).acting;
  near(exhaust.exchangeM3h, 180, 1e-9);
  assert.equal(exhaust.filterPa, 0);
  const none = step(at(1), OFF, F, 0).acting;
  near(none.exchangeM3h, 0.3 * 60, 1e-9, 'the leakage');
  assert.equal(none.filterPa, 0);
  near(step(at(5), { ...OFF, intake: true }, { ...F, fanFlow: 10 }, 0).acting.exchangeM3h, 18, 1e-9, 'never below the leakage');
});

test('without filter settings the fans move fanFlow as before and nothing loads', () => {
  const r = run(initialState(BASE), PAIR, BASE, 3600);
  assert.equal(r.acting.exchangeM3h, 600);
  assert.equal(r.acting.filterPa, 0);
  assert.equal(r.acting.intakeShare, 1);
  assert.equal(r.acting.unfilteredM3h, 0);
  assert.equal(r.state.Lf, 0);
});

test('the filter loads with the air it passes, not with the time', () => {
  const p = { ...F, filterLifeDays: 1 }; // 1 day x 24 h x 600 m³/h = 14 400 m³ to the change pressure
  const pair = run(at(0, p), PAIR, p, 3600).state.Lf;
  near(pair, 600 / 14400, 0.0005, 'about one hour of air');
  const intake = run(at(0, p), { ...OFF, intake: true }, p, 3600).state.Lf;
  near(intake, (0.3 * 600) / 14400, 0.0002);
  assert.equal(run(at(0, p), { ...OFF, exhaust: true }, p, 3600).state.Lf, 0);
  assert.equal(run(at(0, p), OFF, p, 3600).state.Lf, 0);
  let half = run(at(0, p), PAIR, p, 1800).state;
  half = run(half, OFF, p, 1800).state;
  near(half.Lf / pair, 0.5, 0.01, 'half the run time, half the loading');
  near(run(at(0, p), PAIR, p, 3600, 3600).state.Lf, pair, 1e-9, 'the step length does not matter');
  assert.equal(run(at(0, { ...p, fanFlow: 0 }), PAIR, { ...p, fanFlow: 0 }, 3600).state.Lf, 0, 'no fan flow, no loading');
});

test('30 days of the pair at 150 m³/h: loading about 0.82, intake share about 0.69', () => {
  const p = { ...F, fanFlow: 150 };
  const { state, acting } = run(at(0, p), PAIR, p, 30 * 86400, 3600);
  near(state.Lf, 0.817, 0.005); // dL/dt = f / 30 days gives L + 0.275 L² = 1
  near(acting.intakeShare, 0.69, 0.01);
});

test('a dirtier filter means less exchange: CO2 is higher after the same purge', () => {
  const s0 = (Lf) => ({ ...initialState(F), CO2: 2000, Lf });
  const clean = run(s0(0), PAIR, F, 600).state.CO2;
  const dirty = run(s0(1), PAIR, F, 600).state.CO2;
  assert.ok(dirty > clean + 20, `${dirty} vs ${clean}`);
});

test('the intake motor heat follows the filter pressure', () => {
  const s = at(1);
  const a = step(s, { ...OFF, intake: true }, { ...F, outdoorT: s.T, outdoorRH: rhOf(s.T, s.W) }, 5).acting;
  near(a.machineKW, 0.06 * fanWattFactor(150 * F_FINAL, 40, 200), 1e-9);
  near(step(at(0), { ...OFF, intake: true }, F, 5).acting.machineKW, 0.06, 1e-9, 'clean: the rated 60 W');
});

test('fanWattFactor: 1 when clean, 1.15 at the fan maximum, never below 1, 1 for bad values', () => {
  assert.equal(fanWattFactor(40, 40, 200), 1);
  near(fanWattFactor(200, 40, 200), 1.15, 1e-12);
  near(fanWattFactor(120, 40, 200), 1.075, 1e-12);
  assert.equal(fanWattFactor(0, 40, 200), 1);
  assert.equal(fanWattFactor(100, undefined, 200), 1);
  assert.equal(fanWattFactor(NaN, 40, 200), 1);
  assert.equal(fanWattFactor(100, 40, 40), 1);
  assert.equal(fanWattFactor(100, 60, 40), 1);
});

test('electricW: the intake fan draws more on a dirty filter, the exhaust fan does not', () => {
  const run1 = (inputs, filterPa) => electricW({ acting: { acCoolKW: 0, acLatentKW: 0, acHeatKW: 0, filterPa }, inputs: { ac: null, ...inputs }, params: { outdoorT: 27, ...FILTER_DEFAULTS } });
  near(run1({ intake: true }, 200), 69, 1e-9);
  assert.equal(run1({ intake: true }, 40), 60);
  assert.equal(run1({ exhaust: true }, 200), 60);
  assert.equal(electricW({ acting: { acCoolKW: 0, acLatentKW: 0, acHeatKW: 0 }, inputs: { ac: null, intake: true }, params: { outdoorT: 27 } }), 60, 'no filter settings: rated');
});
```

```diff
diff --git a/device/sim-model.js b/device/sim-model.js
index a0c15bf..83e75d0 100644
--- a/device/sim-model.js
+++ b/device/sim-model.js
@@ -1,7 +1,10 @@
 // Pure single-zone climate model for the device app simulation (spec 2026-10-19 section 3).
-// No I/O, no clock. State { T, W, CO2, Tm, Hs }: air °C, air water g/m3, ppm, the room's mass (blocks, racks, the inner
-// layer of floor and walls) °C, and the water held by its surfaces as an RH in % (above 100: free water on them).
+// No I/O, no clock. State { T, W, CO2, Tm, Hs, Lf }: air °C, air water g/m3, ppm, the room's mass (blocks, racks, the inner
+// layer of floor and walls) °C, the water held by its surfaces as an RH in % (above 100: free water on them), and the
+// intake filter's loading (0 clean, 1 at its change pressure).
+
+import { fanWattFactor } from './sim-power.js';
 
 const SUB = 5;                 // max sub-step, seconds
 const LEAK_PER_H = 0.3;        // vol/h
 const SINGLE_FAN = 0.3;        // one fan of the pair alone moves this share of the pair's flow (the rest is blocked by the leaks)
@@ -46,8 +49,24 @@ const HUMIDIFIER_W_PER_LH = 40; // ultrasonic humidifier
 const DEHUM_COIL = 5;
 const DEHUM_MAX = 1.2;
 const dehumShare = (W) => clamp((W - satW(DEHUM_COIL)) / (wOf(30, 80) - satW(DEHUM_COIL)), 0, DEHUM_MAX);
 
+// Intake filter (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 2): the fan's curve is a straight line from (0, fanMaxPa) through the
+// clean operating point (fanFlow, filterCleanPa); the filter's drop at fanFlow rises from clean to final with its loading,
+// in proportion to the flow. Where the two lines meet gives the intake's share of fanFlow. The filter loads with the air it
+// passes: filterLifeDays of fanFlow takes it to its change pressure. A room without these settings has no filter.
+export const FILTER_DEFAULTS = { fanMaxPa: 200, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 150, filterLifeDays: 30 };
+export const hasFilter = (p) => [p.fanMaxPa, p.filterCleanPa, p.filterFinalPa].every(Number.isFinite) && p.filterLifeDays > 0;
+
+export function filterOf(params, Lf) {
+  if (!hasFilter(params)) return { share: 1, resistancePa: 0 };
+  const { fanMaxPa, filterCleanPa, filterFinalPa } = params;
+  const resistancePa = filterCleanPa + (filterFinalPa - filterCleanPa) * Lf;
+  return { share: fanMaxPa / (fanMaxPa - filterCleanPa + resistancePa), resistancePa };
+}
+
+export const changeFilter = (state) => ({ ...state, Lf: 0 });
+
 const clamp = (x, lo, hi) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, x));
 
 export function satW(T) {
   const es = 0.6108 * Math.exp((17.27 * T) / (T + 237.3)); // kPa
@@ -86,25 +105,44 @@ export function outdoorAt({ outdoorHigh, outdoorLow, outdoorDew }, clockS) {
 }
 
 export function initialState(params) {
   const T = params.outdoorT, RH = clamp(params.outdoorRH, 0, 100);
-  return { T, W: wOf(T, RH), CO2: CO2_OUT, Tm: T, Hs: RH };
+  return { T, W: wOf(T, RH), CO2: CO2_OUT, Tm: T, Hs: RH, Lf: 0 };
 }
 
-// A state from before the mass and the surfaces: they start level with the air.
+// A state from before the mass and the surfaces: they start level with the air; from before the filter: a clean filter.
 const full = (s) => ({
   T: s.T, W: s.W, CO2: s.CO2,
   Tm: Number.isFinite(s.Tm) ? s.Tm : s.T,
   Hs: Number.isFinite(s.Hs) ? s.Hs : rhOf(s.T, s.W),
+  Lf: Number.isFinite(s.Lf) ? s.Lf : 0,
 });
 
-function exchangeOf(inputs, params) {
+// share: the intake's share of fanFlow through its filter. With both fans the exhaust keeps pulling and the shortfall comes
+// in through the leaks.
+function exchangeOf(inputs, params, share = 1) {
   const leak = LEAK_PER_H * params.volume;
-  if (inputs.intake && inputs.exhaust) return Math.max(leak, params.fanFlow);
-  if (inputs.intake || inputs.exhaust) return Math.max(leak, SINGLE_FAN * params.fanFlow);
+  if (inputs.intake && inputs.exhaust) return Math.max(leak, (params.fanFlow * (1 + share)) / 2);
+  if (inputs.intake) return Math.max(leak, SINGLE_FAN * params.fanFlow * share);
+  if (inputs.exhaust) return Math.max(leak, SINGLE_FAN * params.fanFlow);
   return leak;
 }
 
+// The filter now: its pressure while the intake runs, the air through it, and the air the exhaust pulls in through the leaks.
+function filterNow(inputs, params, Lf) {
+  const { share, resistancePa } = filterOf(params, Lf);
+  const flow = params.fanFlow;
+  return {
+    share,
+    filterPa: inputs.intake ? resistancePa * share : 0,
+    throughM3h: inputs.intake ? (inputs.exhaust ? flow : SINGLE_FAN * flow) * share : 0,
+    unfilteredM3h: inputs.intake && inputs.exhaust ? (flow * (1 - share)) / 2 : 0,
+  };
+}
+
+// The intake motor's heat: more against a dirtier filter.
+const intakeW = (filter, params) => INTAKE_W * fanWattFactor(filter.filterPa, params.filterCleanPa, params.fanMaxPa);
+
 // The aircon now: total capacity P (kW) and its sensible share, or heat.
 function acOf(ac, params, T, W) {
   const none = { coolKW: 0, shr: 1, heatKW: 0, capKW: 0 };
   if (!ac) return none;
@@ -129,11 +167,12 @@ function substep(s, inputs, params, dt) {
   const capAir = volume * AIR_CP * AIR_CONTENTS;                        // kJ/K
   const capMass = volume * MASS_KJ_PER_M3 + blocks * BLOCK_KJ;          // kJ/K
   const uaMass = (volume * SURFACE_W_PER_M3 + blocks * BLOCK_SURFACE_W) / 1000; // kW/K
   const outW = wOf(outdoorT, outdoorRH);
-  let { T, W, CO2, Tm, Hs } = s;
+  let { T, W, CO2, Tm, Hs, Lf } = s;
 
-  const exchangeM3h = exchangeOf(inputs, params);
+  const filter = filterNow(inputs, params, Lf);
+  const exchangeM3h = exchangeOf(inputs, params, filter.share);
 
   // Humidifier: evaporation stops at saturation, counting the cooling it causes (a cooler air holds less); its motor

 }

 function nominal(inputs, params, s) {

     blocksKW: (params.blocks * (params.co2PerBlock * RESP_KJ_PER_ML - BLOCK_GH * dryness * LATENT)) / 3600,
     sunKW: ((params.sunW ?? 0) * (params.sun ?? 0)) / 1000,
-    machineKW: ((inputs.circulation ? CIRC_W : 0) + (inputs.intake ? INTAKE_W : 0) + (inputs.humidifier ? (HUMIDIFIER_W_PER_LH * humOn) / 1000 : 0)) / 1000,
+    machineKW: ((inputs.circulation ? CIRC_W : 0) + (inputs.intake ? intakeW(filter, params) : 0) + (inputs.humidifier ? (HUMIDIFIER_W_PER_LH * humOn) / 1000 : 0)) / 1000,
     surfaceGH: 0,
     condensedGH: 0,
     dehumidifierGH: inputs.dehumidifier && (params.dehumidifierLday ?? 0) > 0 ? (params.dehumidifierLday * 1000 / 24) * dehumShare(s.W) : 0,
     dehumidifierKW: inputs.dehumidifier && (params.dehumidifierLday ?? 0) > 0 ? (params.dehumidifierW ?? 0) / 1000 : 0,
+    filterPa: filter.filterPa,
+    intakeShare: filter.share,
+    unfilteredM3h: filter.unfilteredM3h,
+    filterLoad: s.Lf,
   };
 }
diff --git a/device/sim-power.js b/device/sim-power.js
index 6f3d896..959cc8a 100644
--- a/device/sim-power.js
+++ b/device/sim-power.js
@@ -1,6 +1,7 @@
 // The electrical power a simulated room draws (W), from what its machines do this step. Pure, no I/O.
 // Aircon: the heat it moves divided by its COP, which falls on a hot day; plus its indoor fan while it is working.
+// Intake fan: more on a dirty filter (fanWattFactor).
 
 const AC_FAN_W = 40;
 const MOTOR_W = 60;            // intake, exhaust and circulation fan
 const HUMIDIFIER_W_PER_LH = 40;
@@ -9,16 +10,23 @@ const clamp = (x, lo, hi) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, x));
 
 // 3.6 at 27 °C outdoors, 3.1 at 35 °C, never outside 2 to 5.
 export const copAt = (outdoorT) => clamp(3.6 - 0.06 * (outdoorT - 27), 2, 5);
 
+// A fixed-speed intake fan against a dirtier filter (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 6): its rated power with a clean filter,
+// up to 1.15 times at the fan's maximum pressure, never below rated. 1 when the filter settings are missing or do not fit.
+export function fanWattFactor(pa, cleanPa, maxPa) {
+  if (![pa, cleanPa, maxPa].every(Number.isFinite) || maxPa <= cleanPa) return 1;
+  return Math.max(1, 1 + (0.15 * (pa - cleanPa)) / (maxPa - cleanPa));
+}
+
 // acting: what the model reported (kW); inputs: the machines (ac is null when off or faulty); params: the room's
 // settings with the outdoor air now. The humidifier's draw follows its setting while it runs; the dehumidifier's is its rated power.
 export function electricW({ acting, inputs, params }) {
   const cop = copAt(params.outdoorT ?? 27);
   const heat = ((acting?.acCoolKW ?? 0) + (acting?.acLatentKW ?? 0) + (acting?.acHeatKW ?? 0)) * 1000;
   let w = heat / cop;
   if (inputs.ac) w += AC_FAN_W;
-  if (inputs.intake) w += MOTOR_W;
+  if (inputs.intake) w += MOTOR_W * fanWattFactor(acting?.filterPa ?? 0, params.filterCleanPa, params.fanMaxPa);
   if (inputs.exhaust) w += MOTOR_W;
   if (inputs.circulation) w += MOTOR_W;
   if (inputs.humidifier) w += (HUMIDIFIER_W_PER_LH * (params.humidifierGH ?? 0)) / 1000;
   if (inputs.dehumidifier) w += (acting?.dehumidifierKW ?? 0) * 1000;
```

Notes: `sim-model.js` now imports `fanWattFactor` from `sim-power.js` (which imports nothing, so no cycle). A room without the filter settings (`hasFilter` false) behaves exactly as before: share 1, `Lf` stays 0, intake 60 W. The pair's exchange is `fanFlow × (1 + share) / 2`; loading is per sub-step from the air through the filter, so the step length does not matter.

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';

function thrown(fn) {
  try { fn(); } catch (err) { return err; }
  assert.fail('expected a throw');
}

// ---- The filter pressure sensor ----

test('a filter pressure sensor starts at 40 Pa and is edited like other devices', () => {
  const s = createSensors({});
  s.add({ address: 9, kind: 'dp' });
  assert.deepEqual(s.get(9), { address: 9, kind: 'dp', pa: 40, answering: true });
  s.update(9, { pa: 123.45 });
  assert.equal(s.get(9).pa, 123.5);
  s.update(9, { pa: -12.3 });
  assert.equal(s.get(9).pa, -12.3);
});

test('its pressure has limits and it has no other values; other kinds are named', () => {
  const s = createSensors({});
  s.add({ address: 9, kind: 'dp' });
  s.add({ address: 8, kind: 'trh' });
  let err = thrown(() => s.update(9, { pa: 2000.1 }));
  assert.deepEqual([err.status, err.field, err.message], [400, 'pa', 'Pressure must be a number from -50 to 2000 Pa']);
  err = thrown(() => s.update(9, { pa: -50.1 }));
  assert.equal(err.status, 400);
  err = thrown(() => s.update(9, { t: 20 }));
  assert.deepEqual([err.status, err.field, err.message], [400, 't', 'A filter pressure sensor has no t value']);
  err = thrown(() => s.update(8, { pa: 5 }));
  assert.deepEqual([err.status, err.field], [400, 'pa']);
  err = thrown(() => s.add({ address: 10, kind: 'flow' }));
  assert.deepEqual([err.status, err.field, err.message], [400, 'kind', 'Kind must be trh, co2, relay, ac, meter or dp']);
});

test('a filter pressure sensor is saved in the state file and comes back', () => {
  const statePath = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'mf-dp-')), 'state.json');
  const a = createSensors({ statePath });
  a.add({ address: 9, kind: 'dp' });
  a.update(9, { pa: 88 });
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(readFileSync(statePath, 'utf8')), [{ address: 9, kind: 'dp', pa: 88, answering: true }]);
  assert.deepEqual(createSensors({ statePath }).get(9), { address: 9, kind: 'dp', pa: 88, answering: true });
  writeFileSync(statePath, JSON.stringify([{ address: 9, kind: 'dp', pa: 99999, answering: true }, { address: 10, kind: 'dp', pa: 12 }]));
  const c = createSensors({ statePath });
  assert.equal(c.get(9), undefined, 'a bad value skips the entry');
  assert.equal(c.get(10).pa, 12);
});

test('the simulation writes the pressure, clamped to the limits', () => {
  const s = createSensors({});
  s.add({ address: 9, kind: 'dp' });
  s.simWrite(9, { pa: 96.789 });
  assert.equal(s.get(9).pa, 96.8);
  s.simWrite(9, { pa: 5000 });
  assert.equal(s.get(9).pa, 2000);
  s.simWrite(9, { pa: 'x' });
  assert.equal(s.get(9).pa, 2000);
});

// ---- Modbus ----

async function ask(server, frames) {
  await new Promise((resolve) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve));
  const socket = net.connect(server.address().port, '127.0.0.1');
  try {
    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => { socket.once('connect', resolve); socket.once('error', reject); });
    const replies = [];
    for (const f of frames) {
      const got = new Promise((resolve) => socket.once('data', resolve));
      socket.write(f);
      replies.push(await Promise.race([got, new Promise((r) => setTimeout(() => r(null), 300))]));
    }
    return replies;
  } finally {
    socket.destroy();
    await new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve));
  }
}

function frame(unit, fn, start, qty) {
  const b = Buffer.alloc(12);
  b.writeUInt16BE(1, 0);
  b.writeUInt16BE(6, 4);
  b.writeUInt8(unit, 6);
  b.writeUInt8(fn, 7);
  b.writeUInt16BE(start, 8);
  b.writeUInt16BE(qty, 10);
  return b;
}

test('a filter pressure sensor answers function 04 register 0 with Pa x 10, signed', async () => {
  const s = createSensors({});
  s.add({ address: 9, kind: 'dp' });
  s.update(9, { pa: 123.4 });
  s.add({ address: 10, kind: 'dp' });
  s.update(10, { pa: -12.3 });
  const [up, down] = await ask(createModbusServer(s), [frame(9, 4, 0, 1), frame(10, 4, 0, 1)]);
  assert.deepEqual([...up.subarray(7, 9)], [4, 2]);
  assert.equal(up.readInt16BE(9), 1234);
  assert.equal(down.readInt16BE(9), -123);
  assert.equal(down.readUInt16BE(9), 0xff85);
});

test('any other range is an illegal address, any other function an illegal function; a silent one does not answer', async () => {
  const s = createSensors({});
  s.add({ address: 9, kind: 'dp' });
  const replies = await ask(createModbusServer(s), [frame(9, 4, 1, 1), frame(9, 4, 0, 2), frame(9, 3, 0, 1)]);
  assert.deepEqual(replies.map((r) => [r[7], r[8]]), [[0x84, 2], [0x84, 2], [0x83, 1]]);
  s.update(9, { answering: false });
  const [silent] = await ask(createModbusServer(s), [frame(9, 4, 0, 1)]);
  assert.equal(silent, null);
});
```

```diff
+test('the runner with the real model: a running intake shows the filter pressure', () => {
+  const sensors = createSensors({ spinUpMs: 0 });
+  sensors.add({ address: 4, kind: 'relay' });
+  sensors.add({ address: 9, kind: 'dp' });
+  const sims = createSims({ sensors });
+  const { id } = sims.add({ name: 'Real' });
+  sims.update(id, { enabled: true, relay: 4, filterProbe: 9 });
+  sensors.writeCoil(4, 0, true); // intake
+  sensors.writeCoil(4, 1, true); // exhaust
+  createSimulation({ sensors, sims, tickMs: 0 }).tick();
+  near(sensors.get(9).pa, 40, 0.1, 'a clean filter at fanFlow');
+  sims.setState(id, { ...sims.get(id).state, Lf: 1 }, null);
+  createSimulation({ sensors, sims, tickMs: 0 }).tick();
+  near(sensors.get(9).pa, 150 * (200 / 310), 0.1);
+  sensors.writeCoil(4, 0, false);
+  createSimulation({ sensors, sims, tickMs: 0 }).tick();
+  assert.equal(sensors.get(9).pa, 0, 'no pressure with the intake off');
+});
+
+test('the loading and the filter settings survive a restart; old rooms get the defaults, settings that do not fit too', () => {
+  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'mf-dp-sim-'));
+  const statePath = join(dir, 'state.json');
+  const sensors = createSensors({ statePath });
+  sensors.add({ address: 9, kind: 'dp' });
+  const a = createSims({ sensors, statePath: `${statePath}.sim`, model: fake() });
+  const { id } = a.add({ name: 'Kept' });
+  a.update(id, { filterProbe: 9, filterLifeDays: 45 });
+  a.setState(id, { T: 20, W: 8, CO2: 700, Lf: 0.4 }, null);
+  a.persist();
+  const b = createSims({ sensors: createSensors({ statePath }), statePath: `${statePath}.sim`, model: fake() });
+  const again = b.get(id);
+  assert.deepEqual([again.filterProbe, again.filterLifeDays, again.state.Lf], [9, 45, 0.4]);
+  writeFileSync(`${statePath}.sim`, JSON.stringify({ speed: 1, rooms: [{ id: 1, name: 'Old', fanFlow: 700 }, { id: 2, name: 'Odd', fanMaxPa: 100, filterCleanPa: 120, filterFinalPa: 300 }] }));
+  const [old, odd] = createSims({ sensors: createSensors({}), statePath: `${statePath}.sim`, model: fake() }).list();
+  assert.deepEqual([old.fanMaxPa, old.filterCleanPa, old.filterFinalPa, old.filterLifeDays, old.filterProbe], [200, 40, 150, 30, null]);
+  assert.deepEqual([odd.fanMaxPa, odd.filterCleanPa, odd.filterFinalPa], [200, 40, 150]);
+});
+
+test('POST /api/sims/:id/change-filter changes the filter; other methods and unknown rooms are 404', async () => {
+  const device = await startDevice({ modbusPort: 0, httpPort: 0, spinUpMs: 0, simTickMs: 0, model: fake() });
+  try {
+    const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${device.httpPort}`;
+    const { id } = device.sims.add({ name: 'Http' });
+    device.sims.setState(id, { T: 24, W: 9, CO2: 900, Lf: 0.9 }, null);
+    const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/sims/${id}/change-filter`, { method: 'POST' });
+    assert.equal(res.status, 200);
+    assert.equal((await res.json()).state.Lf, 0);
+    assert.equal((await fetch(`${base}/api/sims/${id}/change-filter`, { method: 'GET' })).status, 404);
+    assert.equal((await fetch(`${base}/api/sims/99/change-filter`, { method: 'POST' })).status, 404);
+  } finally {
+    await device.close();
+  }
+});
```

Notes: the four `PARAMS` entries keep the one-line `key: { min: …, max: …, whole: …, … value: N }` shape that `tests/web-simulate-math.test.js` reads with a regular expression (Part C may add these keys to the stepper's `PHYSICS_FIELDS`). `changeFilter` sets `state.Lf = 0` itself, so a test model without `changeFilter` still works; a reset keeps `Lf` (it puts back the air, not the filter). A saved room from before this change gets the default filter settings, so the owner's simulated rooms start clogging after the upgrade: press **Change filter** now and then.

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { PARAMS } from '../device/sim-store.js';

// The device page is plain browser code: these checks read its source, so a new device setting or kind is not left off it.
const app = readFileSync(new URL('../device/web/app.js', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
const html = readFileSync(new URL('../device/web/index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');

test('every simulated room setting has an input on the device page', () => {
  for (const key of Object.keys(PARAMS)) assert.match(app, new RegExp(`\\['${key}', '`), key);
});

test('the device page knows the filter pressure sensor: add form, table, link and its simulated value', () => {
  assert.match(html, /<option value="dp">Filter pressure sensor<\/option>/);
  assert.match(app, /dp: 'Filter pressure sensor'/);
  assert.match(app, /\['filterProbe', 'Filter pressure sensor'\]/);
  assert.match(app, /filterProbe: 'dp'/);
  assert.match(app, /dp: \['pa'\]/);
});

test('the sim card has a Change filter button that posts to the room', () => {
  assert.match(app, /textContent: 'Change filter'/);
  assert.match(app, /\/api\/sims\/\$\{id\}\/change-filter/);
});
```

Expected: `# tests 1110`, `# fail 0`

Look at the page without touching the owner's running device app: start a throwaway device on random ports with a temporary state file and screenshot it. Run `mkdir -p /tmp/filter-a-check`, save the script below as `/tmp/filter-a-check/shot.mjs`, run `node --disable-warning=ExperimentalWarning /tmp/filter-a-check/shot.mjs "$PWD"` from the repository root, then open `/tmp/filter-a-check/device.png`. The Chrome call must be asynchronous: the device's HTTP server runs in the same process, and a blocking call would stop it from answering.

```js
// Starts a throwaway device on random ports with a temp state file, sets up a simulated room with a filter probe,
// screenshots the device page with headless Chrome, and stops. Argument: the worktree root.
import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { execFile } from 'node:child_process';
import { promisify } from 'node:util';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const root = process.argv[2];
const { startDevice } = await import(pathToFileURL(join(root, 'device/server.js')).href);
const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'mf-a-shot-'));
const device = await startDevice({ modbusPort: 0, httpPort: 0, statePath: join(dir, 'state.json'), spinUpMs: 0, simTickMs: 200 });
try {
  device.sensors.add({ address: 4, kind: 'relay' });
  device.sensors.add({ address: 9, kind: 'dp' });
  const { id } = device.sims.add({ name: 'Filter check' });
  device.sims.update(id, { enabled: true, relay: 4, filterProbe: 9, fanFlow: 150 });
  device.sims.setState(id, { ...device.sims.get(id).state, Lf: 1 }, null);
  device.sensors.writeCoil(4, 0, true);
  device.sensors.writeCoil(4, 1, true);
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1500));
  await promisify(execFile)('google-chrome-stable', ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--no-sandbox', '--hide-scrollbars', '--window-size=1300,2400',
    '--virtual-time-budget=4000', `--screenshot=${join('/tmp/filter-a-check', 'device.png')}`, `http://127.0.0.1:${device.httpPort}/`], { timeout: 60000 });
  console.log('room', JSON.stringify(device.sims.get(id).acting), 'dp', device.sensors.get(9).pa);
} finally {
  await device.close();
}
```

Expected: it prints `filterPa` ≈ 96.77, `intakeShare` ≈ 0.645, `unfilteredM3h` ≈ 26.6 and `dp 96.8`; the picture shows the device row "Filter pressure sensor · ΔP Pa 96.8 · simulated by Filter check", the sim card buttons Reset / Change filter / Remove, the Filter pressure sensor link, the four filter inputs, the line "Filter 100 % of the way to its change pressure · 97 Pa · intake at 65 % of its clean flow" and "Air exchange 123 m³/h · 27 m³/h of it unfiltered, through the leaks".

## Part B: the farm server

Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md` sections 3, 4, 5, 6 (real room) and 9. Names: `contract.md` next to this file.
The plan runs part A, then part B. Every code block below was replayed on a clone of `v2` (ee4d30a) with part A's four
commits applied first: the suite went from 1110 tests (after part A) to 1155, all passing.

Conventions for every task:

- Run tests with `node --disable-warning=ExperimentalWarning --test <files>`; the whole suite with `npm test`.
- New files are given whole. Changes to existing files are unified diffs against the file as the previous task left it; apply
  them by hand or with `git apply` (save the block to a file first).
- Commit messages follow the repo (a plain sentence, no prefix, no trailers).
- Never start, stop or restart the owner's apps (`npm start`, `npm run device`), never open `data/*.db`.

Prerequisite: Task A1 (device/sim-power.js exports fanWattFactor). Nothing to do here.

### Task B1: Room settings: the filter fields and the filter pressure sensor address

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/rooms-filter.test.js`
- Modify: `server/rooms.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/rooms.test.js`

`server/rooms.js` gets the four filter settings (`FILTER`, whole numbers, left out = kept or default) and `dpAddress` as a sixth
address (so the in-room clash check covers it), plus the two cross checks. A value left out is compared as its default:
the room pages send all four (part C). The `good` fixture of `tests/rooms.test.js` gains `dpAddress: null`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `tests/rooms-filter.test.js`

```js
// The room's filter settings and filter pressure sensor address (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md section 3).

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { validateRoom, FILTER } from '../server/rooms.js';

const BASE = { name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5 };

test('the four filter settings with their defaults, ranges and units', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(FILTER.map((f) => [f.field, f.default, f.min, f.max, f.unit]), [
    ['fanMaxPa', 200, 20, 2000, 'Pa'],
    ['filterCleanPa', 40, 5, 1000, 'Pa'],
    ['filterFinalPa', 150, 5, 2000, 'Pa'],
    ['filterChangeDays', 30, 0, 365, 'days'],
  ]);
});

test('filter settings left out stay out (an update keeps the stored ones, a new room gets the defaults)', () => {
  const { room } = validateRoom(BASE);
  for (const f of FILTER) assert.equal(f.field in room, false, f.field);
  assert.equal(room.dpAddress, null);
});

test('given filter settings are kept; null counts as left out', () => {
  const { room } = validateRoom({ ...BASE, fanMaxPa: 300, filterCleanPa: 30, filterFinalPa: 120, filterChangeDays: 0, dpAddress: 9 });
  assert.deepEqual([room.fanMaxPa, room.filterCleanPa, room.filterFinalPa, room.filterChangeDays, room.dpAddress], [300, 30, 120, 0, 9]);
  assert.equal('fanMaxPa' in validateRoom({ ...BASE, fanMaxPa: null }).room, false);
});

test('each filter setting must be a whole number in its range', () => {
  const cases = [
    ['fanMaxPa', 19, 'Intake fan maximum pressure must be a whole number from 20 to 2000 Pa'],
    ['fanMaxPa', 200.5, 'Intake fan maximum pressure must be a whole number from 20 to 2000 Pa'],
    ['filterCleanPa', 1001, 'Clean filter pressure drop must be a whole number from 5 to 1000 Pa'],
    ['filterFinalPa', 2001, 'Filter change pressure must be a whole number from 5 to 2000 Pa'],
    ['filterChangeDays', -1, 'Change the filter every must be a whole number from 0 to 365 days'],
    ['filterChangeDays', '30', 'Change the filter every must be a whole number from 0 to 365 days'],
  ];
  for (const [field, value, error] of cases) assert.deepEqual(validateRoom({ ...BASE, [field]: value }), { error, field });
});

test('the fan must push above the clean filter, and the change pressure must be above the clean one', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(validateRoom({ ...BASE, fanMaxPa: 40, filterCleanPa: 40 }), {
    error: "Fan maximum pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop", field: 'fanMaxPa',
  });
  assert.deepEqual(validateRoom({ ...BASE, filterCleanPa: 60, filterFinalPa: 60 }), {
    error: "Filter change pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop", field: 'filterFinalPa',
  });
  // A value left out is compared as its default (the pages send all four).
  assert.equal(validateRoom({ ...BASE, filterCleanPa: 150 }).field, 'filterFinalPa');
  assert.equal(validateRoom({ ...BASE, filterCleanPa: 190, filterFinalPa: 300 }).room.filterCleanPa, 190);
});

test('the filter pressure sensor address: 1 to 247, and not one of the room\'s other addresses', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(validateRoom({ ...BASE, dpAddress: 0 }), { error: 'Filter pressure sensor address must be a whole number from 1 to 247', field: 'dpAddress' });
  assert.deepEqual(validateRoom({ ...BASE, trhAddress: 5, dpAddress: 5 }), { error: 'The devices in a room need different addresses', field: 'dpAddress' });
});
```

```diff
+    return { error: "Filter change pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop", field: 'filterFinalPa' };

     const { field } = ADDRESSES[i];
```

### Task B2: Database 14: room columns, the filter tables and their methods

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/db-filter.test.js`
- Modify: `server/db.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/db.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/db-usage.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/db-machines.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/db-acfan.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/controller-fresh-air.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/db-safety.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/db-programs.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/db-control.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/dehumidifier-review.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/db-settings.test.js`

Version 14. The upgrade only adds the columns when `rooms.fan_max_pa` is missing and creates the tables with `IF NOT EXISTS`:
several older upgrade tests make a current file, set `user_version` back by hand and open it again (as upgrade 12 does with
`has(...)`). Existing rooms get `filter_changed_at` = the upgrade time. `filter_readings` gets the meter's three indexes
(status by id, last good by id, a covering time window of the good readings) instead of the spec's single `(room_id, at)`.
After the change, the existing tests that pin `user_version` 13 or list a room's fields need the edits shown with the
implementation (version 14; the six new room fields; after an upgrade `filterChangedAt` is the upgrade time, read back from
the room).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `tests/db-filter.test.js`

```js
// The filter in the database (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md sections 3, 4, 5 and 9): room fields, the pressure sensor's
// readings, the filter changes, upgrade 13 to 14.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { openDb, AddressTakenError } from '../server/db.js';

const ROOM = { name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5 };

function fresh(t) {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  t.after(() => db.close());
  return db;
}

test('a new room has the filter defaults, no sensor and its filter changed when it was made', (t) => {
  const db = fresh(t);
  const room = db.createRoom(ROOM, 5000);
  assert.deepEqual(
    [room.fanMaxPa, room.filterCleanPa, room.filterFinalPa, room.filterChangeDays, room.filterChangedAt, room.dpAddress],
    [200, 40, 150, 30, 5000, null],
  );
});

test('given filter settings are stored; an update without them keeps them and the change time', (t) => {
  const db = fresh(t);
  const id = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, fanMaxPa: 300, filterCleanPa: 30, filterFinalPa: 120, filterChangeDays: 14, dpAddress: 9 }, 5000).id;
  db.updateRoom(id, { ...ROOM, dpAddress: 9 }, 6000);
  const room = db.getRoom(id);
  assert.deepEqual(
    [room.fanMaxPa, room.filterCleanPa, room.filterFinalPa, room.filterChangeDays, room.filterChangedAt, room.dpAddress],
    [300, 30, 120, 14, 5000, 9],
  );
  db.updateRoom(id, { ...ROOM, filterChangeDays: 0, dpAddress: null }, 7000);
  assert.deepEqual([db.getRoom(id).filterChangeDays, db.getRoom(id).dpAddress, db.getRoom(id).fanMaxPa], [0, null, 300]);
});

test('the filter pressure sensor address is unique like the other addresses', (t) => {
  const db = fresh(t);
  db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, trhAddress: 5, dpAddress: 6 });
  assert.throws(() => db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, name: 'Room 2', dpAddress: 5 }), (e) => e instanceof AddressTakenError && e.field === 'dpAddress');
  assert.throws(() => db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, name: 'Room 2', co2Address: 6 }), (e) => e instanceof AddressTakenError && e.field === 'co2Address');
});

test('readings are stored only while the room has that sensor address; listFilterSensors lists them', (t) => {
  const db = fresh(t);
  const a = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, dpAddress: 7 }).id;
  db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, name: 'Room 2' });
  assert.deepEqual(db.listFilterSensors(), [{ roomId: a, address: 7 }]);
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: a, address: 7, at: 100, ok: true, pa: 55.5 });
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: a, address: 8, at: 101, ok: true, pa: 60 }); // not its address
  assert.deepEqual(db.getFilterSensor(a).readings, [{ at: 100, ok: true, pa: 55.5 }]);
});

test('the sensor status like the energy meter: not wired, no data, online, timeout, offline', (t) => {
  const db = fresh(t);
  const id = db.createRoom(ROOM).id;
  assert.deepEqual(db.getFilterSensor(id), { kind: 'dp', address: null, status: 'not-wired', failuresInRow: 0, lastOk: null, readings: [] });
  db.updateRoom(id, { ...ROOM, dpAddress: 7 });
  assert.equal(db.getFilterSensor(id).status, 'no-data');
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: 100, ok: true, pa: 50 });
  assert.equal(db.getFilterSensor(id).status, 'online');
  assert.deepEqual(db.getFilterSensor(id).lastOk, { at: 100, pa: 50 });
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: 101, ok: false, error: 'Timeout' });
  assert.equal(db.getFilterSensor(id).status, 'timeout');
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: 102, ok: false, pa: 2500, error: 'Out of range' });
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: 103, ok: false, error: 'Timeout' });
  const s = db.getFilterSensor(id);
  assert.deepEqual([s.status, s.failuresInRow, s.lastOk.at], ['offline', 3, 100]);
  assert.deepEqual(s.readings.map((r) => r.pa), [50, null, 2500, null]);
  assert.equal(db.getFilterSensor(999), null);
});

test('filterReadingsWindow: the good readings of the present address in [from, to), oldest first', (t) => {
  const db = fresh(t);
  const id = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, dpAddress: 7 }).id;
  for (const [at, ok, pa] of [[100, true, 40], [200, false, null], [300, true, 45], [400, true, 50]]) {
    db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at, ok, pa });
  }
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterReadingsWindow(id, 100, 400), [{ at: 100, pa: 40 }, { at: 300, pa: 45 }]);
  db.updateRoom(id, { ...ROOM, dpAddress: 8 });
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterReadingsWindow(id, 0, 1000), []);
  db.updateRoom(id, ROOM);
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterReadingsWindow(id, 0, 1000), []);
});

test('markFilterChanged sets the change time and adds a change; filterChanges newest first, at most `limit`', (t) => {
  const db = fresh(t);
  const id = db.createRoom(ROOM, 1000).id;
  assert.equal(db.markFilterChanged(id, 2000, 140.5), true);
  db.markFilterChanged(id, 3000, null);
  assert.equal(db.getRoom(id).filterChangedAt, 3000);
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterChanges(id), [{ at: 3000, paBefore: null }, { at: 2000, paBefore: 140.5 }]);
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterChanges(id, 1), [{ at: 3000, paBefore: null }]);
  assert.equal(db.markFilterChanged(999, 2000, null), false);
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterChanges(999), []);
});

test('deleting a room deletes its filter readings and changes', (t) => {
  const db = fresh(t);
  const id = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, dpAddress: 7 }).id;
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: 100, ok: true, pa: 50 });
  db.markFilterChanged(id, 200, 50);
  db.deleteRoom(id);
  const again = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, dpAddress: 7 }).id; // a new room may get the same id
  assert.deepEqual(db.getFilterSensor(again).readings, []);
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterChanges(again), []);
});

test('upgrade 13 to 14: the filter columns with their defaults, the change time is the upgrade time, the new tables', (t) => {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'filter-'));
  t.after(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  const path = join(dir, 'farm.db');
  const first = openDb(path);
  const id = first.createRoom({ ...ROOM, trhAddress: 1 }, 1000).id;
  first.close();
  // back to a version 13 file
  const raw = new DatabaseSync(path);
  raw.exec(`DROP TABLE filter_readings; DROP TABLE filter_changes;
    ALTER TABLE rooms DROP COLUMN fan_max_pa; ALTER TABLE rooms DROP COLUMN filter_clean_pa;
    ALTER TABLE rooms DROP COLUMN filter_final_pa; ALTER TABLE rooms DROP COLUMN filter_change_days;
    ALTER TABLE rooms DROP COLUMN filter_changed_at; ALTER TABLE rooms DROP COLUMN dp_address;
    PRAGMA user_version = 13;`);
  raw.close();
  const before = Date.now();
  const db = openDb(path);
  t.after(() => db.close());
  const room = db.getRoom(id);
  assert.deepEqual([room.fanMaxPa, room.filterCleanPa, room.filterFinalPa, room.filterChangeDays, room.dpAddress, room.trhAddress],
    [200, 40, 150, 30, null, 1]);
  assert.ok(room.filterChangedAt >= before && room.filterChangedAt <= Date.now());
  db.updateRoom(id, { ...ROOM, dpAddress: 7 });
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: 100, ok: true, pa: 50 });
  db.markFilterChanged(id, 200, 50);
  assert.equal(db.filterChanges(id).length, 1);
  const check = new DatabaseSync(path, { readOnly: true });
  t.after(() => check.close());
  assert.equal(check.prepare('PRAGMA user_version').get().user_version, 14);
});
```

```diff
+    // ---- Filter pressure sensor and filter changes (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 4 and 5) ----
+
+    // Every room with a filter pressure sensor, by room id: [{ roomId, address }].
+    listFilterSensors() {
+      return q.wiredFilters.all().map((r) => ({ roomId: r.roomId, address: r.address }));
+    },
+
+    // One poll of the sensor (pa in Pa; kept on an out-of-range poll too); only while the room still has that address.
+    // A row inside a worked-out usage day makes it stale (its intake estimate uses the day's pressure).
+    addFilterReading({ roomId, address, at, ok, pa = null, error = null }) {
+      q.addFilterReading.run(roomId, address, at, ok ? 1 : 0, pa, error, roomId, address);
+      for (const [k, e] of usageCache.get(roomId) ?? []) if (e.ms && e.end > at) usageCache.get(roomId).delete(k);
+    },
+
+    // The room's sensor like the energy meter of getMeter: { kind: 'dp', address, status, failuresInRow, lastOk, readings },
+    // lastOk and readings carrying { at, pa }. Null when there is no such room.
+    getFilterSensor(roomId, limit = 50) {
+      const room = getRoom(roomId);
+      if (!room) return null;
+      const address = room.dpAddress;
+      const sensor = { kind: 'dp', address, status: 'not-wired', failuresInRow: 0, lastOk: null, readings: [] };
+      if (address === null) return sensor;
+      const rows = q.filterLatest.all(roomId, address, limit);
+      sensor.readings = rows.map((r) => ({ at: r.at, ok: r.ok === 1, pa: r.pa }));
+      if (rows.length === 0) {
+        sensor.status = 'no-data';
+        return sensor;
+      }
+      const ok = q.filterLastOk.get(roomId, address);
+      if (ok) sensor.lastOk = { at: ok.at, pa: ok.pa };
+      sensor.failuresInRow = q.filterFailuresSince.get(roomId, address, ok ? ok.id : 0).n;
+      const n = sensor.failuresInRow;
+      sensor.status = n === 0 ? 'online' : n < FAILS_FOR_OFFLINE ? 'timeout' : 'offline';
+      return sensor;
+    },
+
+    // The good readings of the room's present sensor address in [from, to), oldest first: [{ at, pa }].
+    filterReadingsWindow(roomId, from, to) {
+      const room = q.get.get(roomId);
+      return room?.dp_address == null ? [] : q.filterWindow.all(roomId, room.dp_address, from, to).map((r) => ({ at: r.at, pa: r.pa }));
+    },
+
+    // The filter was changed at `at` (paBefore: the last counted pressure, or null). Returns false when there is no such room.
+    markFilterChanged(roomId, at, paBefore) {
+      if (!q.get.get(roomId)) return false;
+      db.exec('BEGIN');
+      try {
+        q.setFilterChangedAt.run(at, roomId);
+        q.addFilterChange.run(roomId, at, paBefore);
+        db.exec('COMMIT');
+        return true;
+      } catch (err) {
+        db.exec('ROLLBACK');
+        throw err;
+      }
+    },
+
+    // The room's last `limit` filter changes, newest first: [{ at, paBefore }].
+    filterChanges(roomId, limit = 20) {
+      return q.filterChanges.all(roomId, limit).map((r) => ({ at: r.at, paBefore: r.pa_before }));
+    },
+
     // ---- Outdoor probe (spec section 2) ----
 
     // The outdoor address from the settings, or null.
```

### Task B3: Poller: the filter pressure sensor

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/poller-filter.test.js`
- Modify: `server/poller.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/controller-runner.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/poller-machines.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/poller.test.js`
- Modify (tests): `tests/poller-usage.test.js`

The register decode lives in the poller next to the meter's (the Modbus client stays generic), so there is no
`readFilterPressure` in `server/modbus.js`. Per room: T/RH, CO₂, **filter**, meter, machines. Every fake database of the
poller tests gains `listFilterSensors: () => []`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `tests/poller-filter.test.js`

```js
// The poller and the filter pressure sensor (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md section 4). A fake database and Modbus client.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createPoller } from '../server/poller.js';
import { ModbusError } from '../server/modbus.js';

function fakeDb({ sensors = [], meters = [], filters = [] } = {}) {
  const db = {
    filterReadings: [],
    readings: [],
    meterReadings: [],
    listWiredSensors: () => sensors,
    listWiredMachines: () => [],
    listMeters: () => meters,
    listFilterSensors: () => filters,
    outdoorAddress: () => null,
    addReading: (r) => db.readings.push(r),
    addMeterReading: (r) => db.meterReadings.push(r),
    addFilterReading: (r) => db.filterReadings.push(r),
  };
  return db;
}

// The filter sensor (address 7) answers `pa` registers; the meter 0x4366 0x8000 (230.5 W) and 0x4144 0x0000 (12.25 kWh).
function fakeClient(pa = [553]) {
  const calls = [];
  return {
    calls,
    async readRegisters(unit, fn, start, qty) {
      calls.push([unit, fn, start, qty]);
      if (unit === 7) {
        if (pa instanceof Error) throw pa;
        return pa;
      }
      if (start === 0x000c) return [0x4366, 0x8000];
      if (start === 0x0156) return [0x4144, 0x0000];
      return [265, 881];
    },
  };
}

test('the filter sensor is read after the room\'s T/RH and CO2 sensors and before its meter: function 4, register 0, Pa × 10', async () => {
  const db = fakeDb({
    sensors: [{ roomId: 1, kind: 'trh', address: 3 }, { roomId: 1, kind: 'co2', address: 4 }],
    meters: [{ roomId: 1, address: 5 }],
    filters: [{ roomId: 1, address: 7 }],
  });
  const client = fakeClient();
  let clock = 100;
  await createPoller({ db, client, now: () => clock++ }).cycle();
  assert.deepEqual(client.calls.map((c) => c[0]), [3, 4, 7, 5, 5]);
  assert.deepEqual(client.calls[2], [7, 4, 0, 1]);
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterReadings, [{ roomId: 1, address: 7, at: 102, ok: true, pa: 55.3 }]);
});

test('the register is signed: a small negative pressure (sensor zero drift) is kept', async () => {
  const db = fakeDb({ filters: [{ roomId: 2, address: 7 }] });
  await createPoller({ db, client: fakeClient([0xfffb]), now: () => 5 }).cycle();
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterReadings, [{ roomId: 2, address: 7, at: 5, ok: true, pa: -0.5 }]);
});

test('outside −50 to 2000 Pa the poll fails with "Out of range" and the value is kept', async () => {
  for (const [regs, pa] of [[[20001], 2000.1], [[0xfe0b], -50.1]]) {
    const db = fakeDb({ filters: [{ roomId: 1, address: 7 }] });
    await createPoller({ db, client: fakeClient(regs), now: () => 5 }).cycle();
    assert.deepEqual(db.filterReadings, [{ roomId: 1, address: 7, at: 5, ok: false, pa, error: 'Out of range' }]);
  }
  const db = fakeDb({ filters: [{ roomId: 1, address: 7 }] });
  await createPoller({ db, client: fakeClient([20000]), now: () => 5 }).cycle();
  assert.equal(db.filterReadings[0].ok, true, '2000 Pa itself is in range');
});

test('a failed read is a failed poll with the Modbus error', async () => {
  const db = fakeDb({ filters: [{ roomId: 1, address: 7 }] });
  await createPoller({ db, client: fakeClient(new ModbusError('Timeout')), now: () => 5 }).cycle();
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterReadings, [{ roomId: 1, address: 7, at: 5, ok: false, error: 'Timeout' }]);
});

test('a room with only a filter sensor is polled, rooms in id order', async () => {
  const db = fakeDb({ sensors: [{ roomId: 3, kind: 'trh', address: 3 }], filters: [{ roomId: 1, address: 7 }] });
  const client = fakeClient();
  await createPoller({ db, client }).cycle();
  assert.deepEqual(client.calls.map((c) => c[0]), [7, 3]);
});
```

```diff
diff --git a/server/poller.js b/server/poller.js

@@ -1,5 +1,5 @@

-// one reading per poll;
+// The polling cycle: reads the outdoor probe, then every wired sensor, filter pressure sensor and energy meter through the
+// Modbus client and stores one reading per poll;
 // also polls each wired machine (relay module, aircon gateway) and keeps its last polls in memory.
 
 import { ModbusError, registersToFloat } from './modbus.js';
@@ -14,6 +14,10 @@ const KINDS = {
 // Energy meter (SDM120 style): input registers (fn 4), each a float32 over two registers, high word first.
 const METER = { power: { start: 0x000c, qty: 2 }, energy: { start: 0x0156, qty: 2 } };
 
+// Filter pressure sensor (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 4): input register 0 (fn 4), the pressure drop in Pa × 10, signed.
+// A value outside FILTER.min to FILTER.max is a failed poll that keeps the value.
+const FILTER = { fn: 4, start: 0, qty: 1, min: -50, max: 2000 };
+
 // Relay module: coils (fn 1) then current inputs (fn 2). Aircon gateway: settings (fn 3) then room T and error (fn 4).
 const MACHINES = {
   relay: async (client, unit) => ({
@@ -77,6 +81,20 @@ export function createPoller({
     db.addOutdoorReading(reading);
   }
 
+  async function pollFilter({ roomId, address }, began) {
+    const at = now();
+    began(at);
+    const reading = { roomId, address, at };
+    try {
+      const [raw] = await client.readRegisters(address, FILTER.fn, FILTER.start, FILTER.qty);
+      const pa = ((raw << 16) >> 16) / 10;
+      Object.assign(reading, pa >= FILTER.min && pa <= FILTER.max ? { ok: true, pa } : { ok: false, pa, error: 'Out of range' });
+    } catch (err) {
+      Object.assign(reading, { ok: false, error: err instanceof ModbusError ? err.message : 'bad reply' });
+    }
+    db.addFilterReading(reading);
+  }
+
   // Two reads (power, then energy); a failed read, or a value that is not a number, makes the poll a failed one.
   async function pollMeter({ roomId, address }, began) {
     const at = now();
@@ -116,16 +134,21 @@ export function createPoller({

       for (const roomId of roomIds) {
         for (const sensor of sensors.filter((s) => s.roomId === roomId)) {
           if (stopped) return;

+        for (const filter of filters.filter((f) => f.roomId === roomId)) {
+          if (stopped) return;
+          await pollFilter(filter, began);
+        }
         for (const meter of meters.filter((m) => m.roomId === roomId)) {
           if (stopped) return;
           await pollMeter(meter, began);
```

### Task B4: server/filter.js: counted readings, days in use, alarm input, a filter change

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/filter.test.js`
- Create: `server/filter.js`

A reading counts when it is good, at most 120 s old (the alarms) and taken after the intake had been commanded on for
60 s without a break (from the machine log). `changeFilter` takes the last counted reading of the past day as `pa_before`
(the intake is usually switched off to change the filter), clears the three filter alarms and returns the room.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `tests/filter.test.js`

```js
// The filter rules (server/filter.js, spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md sections 4 and 5): which pressure readings count,
// days in use, the alarm input and a filter change.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { openDb } from '../server/db.js';
import { intakeOnThrough, countedPa, meanCountedPa, daysInUse, filterInput, changeFilter, FILTER_KEYS } from '../server/filter.js';

const S = 1000;
const DAY = 86_400_000;
const on = (at) => ({ at, on: true });
const off = (at) => ({ at, on: false });

test('intakeOnThrough: commanded on at `from` and never off up to `to`', () => {
  assert.equal(intakeOnThrough([on(0)], 10 * S, 70 * S), true);
  assert.equal(intakeOnThrough([on(20 * S)], 10 * S, 70 * S), false, 'switched on after `from`');
  assert.equal(intakeOnThrough([on(0), off(30 * S), on(40 * S)], 10 * S, 70 * S), false, 'a break inside');
  assert.equal(intakeOnThrough([on(0), off(80 * S)], 10 * S, 70 * S), true, 'off only after `to`');
  assert.equal(intakeOnThrough([off(0)], 10 * S, 70 * S), false);
  assert.equal(intakeOnThrough([], 10 * S, 70 * S), false);
});

test('countedPa: the newest good reading at most 120 s old, taken after 60 s of intake', () => {
  const log = [on(0)];
  assert.deepEqual(countedPa([{ at: 61 * S, ok: true, pa: 50 }], log, 70 * S), { at: 61 * S, pa: 50 });
  assert.equal(countedPa([{ at: 59 * S, ok: true, pa: 50 }], log, 70 * S), null, 'the fan was still spinning up');
  assert.equal(countedPa([{ at: 61 * S, ok: true, pa: 50 }], log, 182 * S), null, 'too old');
  assert.deepEqual(countedPa([{ at: 61 * S, ok: true, pa: 50 }], log, 181 * S), { at: 61 * S, pa: 50 });
  const readings = [{ at: 70 * S, ok: true, pa: 50 }, { at: 80 * S, ok: false, pa: 3000 }, { at: 90 * S, ok: true, pa: null }];
  assert.deepEqual(countedPa(readings, log, 95 * S), { at: 70 * S, pa: 50 }, 'failed polls and missing values are skipped');
  // switched off and on again: the newer reading is in the spin-up, the older one still counts
  const broken = [on(0), off(100 * S), on(110 * S)];
  assert.deepEqual(countedPa([{ at: 90 * S, pa: 48 }, { at: 120 * S, pa: 5 }], broken, 125 * S), { at: 90 * S, pa: 48 });
  assert.equal(countedPa([], log, 100 * S), null);
});

test('meanCountedPa: the mean of the counted readings, null without one', () => {
  const log = [on(0), off(200 * S)];
  assert.equal(meanCountedPa([{ at: 30 * S, pa: 5 }, { at: 100 * S, pa: 50 }, { at: 150 * S, pa: 60 }, { at: 250 * S, pa: 0 }], log), 55);
  assert.equal(meanCountedPa([{ at: 30 * S, pa: 5 }], log), null);
});

test('daysInUse: days since the filter was changed, never below 0', () => {
  assert.equal(daysInUse({ filterChangedAt: 0 }, 1.5 * DAY), 1.5);
  assert.equal(daysInUse({ filterChangedAt: 2 * DAY }, DAY), 0);
  assert.equal(daysInUse({ filterChangedAt: null }, DAY), 0);
});

function room(t, fields = {}) {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  t.after(() => db.close());
  const r = db.createRoom({ name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5, relayAddress: 3, dpAddress: 7, ...fields }, 0);
  return { db, id: r.id };
}

test('filterInput: the alarm input of a room from its settings, sensor readings and intake log', (t) => {
  const { db, id } = room(t, { filterChangeDays: 14 });
  db.setRelayCommand(id, 'intake', true, 0);
  db.logMachine(id, 'intake', { on: true }, 'auto', 0);
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: 2 * DAY, ok: true, pa: 88 });
  assert.deepEqual(filterInput(db, db.getRoom(id), 2 * DAY + 5 * S), {
    sensor: true, pa: 88, cleanPa: 40, finalPa: 150, changeDays: 14, daysInUse: (2 * DAY + 5 * S) / DAY,
  });
  db.logMachine(id, 'intake', { on: false }, 'auto', 2 * DAY + 10 * S);
  db.logMachine(id, 'intake', { on: true }, 'auto', 2 * DAY + 20 * S);
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: 2 * DAY + 30 * S, ok: true, pa: 2 });
  assert.equal(filterInput(db, db.getRoom(id), 2 * DAY + 35 * S).pa, 88, 'the spin-up reading does not count');
  assert.equal(filterInput(db, db.getRoom(id), 2 * DAY + 200 * S).pa, null, 'nothing counted lately');
  db.updateRoom(id, { name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5, relayAddress: 3 });
  assert.deepEqual(filterInput(db, db.getRoom(id), 2 * DAY).sensor, false);
});

test('changeFilter: the change with the last counted pressure of the past day, and the filter alarms cleared', (t) => {
  const { db, id } = room(t);
  db.logMachine(id, 'intake', { on: true }, 'auto', 0);
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: 5 * DAY, ok: true, pa: 160 });
  db.logMachine(id, 'intake', { on: false }, 'manual', 5 * DAY + 60 * S); // switched off to change the filter
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: 5 * DAY + 120 * S, ok: true, pa: 0 });
  for (const key of FILTER_KEYS) db.raiseAlarm(id, key, 'warning', key, 5 * DAY);
  db.raiseAlarm(id, 'tHigh', 'warning', 'hot', 5 * DAY);
  const r = changeFilter(db, id, 5 * DAY + 600 * S);
  assert.equal(r.filterChangedAt, 5 * DAY + 600 * S);
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterChanges(id), [{ at: 5 * DAY + 600 * S, paBefore: 160 }]);
  assert.deepEqual(db.activeAlarms(id).map((a) => a.key), ['tHigh']);
  assert.equal(changeFilter(db, 999, 0), null);
});

test('changeFilter without a sensor or a counted reading: no pressure before', (t) => {
  const { db, id } = room(t, { dpAddress: null });
  changeFilter(db, id, 1000);
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterChanges(id), [{ at: 1000, paBefore: null }]);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create** `server/filter.js`

```js
// The intake filter (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md sections 4 and 5): which pressure readings count, how long the filter
// has been in use, the input of the filter alarms and a filter change. The rules are pure; filterInput and changeFilter
// read and write the database.

const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
const COUNT_AFTER_MS = 60_000; // a reading counts once the intake has been commanded on this long (the fan is up to speed)
const MAX_AGE_MS = 120_000; // the alarms judge a reading at most this old

export const FILTER_ALARM_DELAY_MS = 10 * 60_000;
export const FILTER_KEYS = ['filterDue', 'filterDirty', 'filterMissing'];

// Whether the intake was commanded on all through [from, to]. log: its machine log rows [{ at, on }], oldest first, the
// first at or before `from` (db.machineLogWindow gives that).
export function intakeOnThrough(log, from, to) {
  const before = log.findLast((r) => r.at <= from);
  if (!before?.on) return false;
  return log.every((r) => r.at <= from || r.at > to || r.on);
}

// The newest good reading [{ at, pa, ok? }] (oldest first) at most maxAgeMs old and taken after the intake had been on
// COUNT_AFTER_MS without a break: { at, pa }, or null. With the fan off the sensor reads about 0 and says nothing.
export function countedPa(readings, intakeLog, now, maxAgeMs = MAX_AGE_MS) {
  for (let i = readings.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const r = readings[i];
    if (now - r.at > maxAgeMs) break;
    if (r.ok === false || r.pa === null || r.pa === undefined) continue;
    if (intakeOnThrough(intakeLog, r.at - COUNT_AFTER_MS, r.at)) return { at: r.at, pa: r.pa };
  }
  return null;
}

// The mean of the readings [{ at, pa }] that count (see countedPa, without the age limit), or null when none does.
export function meanCountedPa(readings, intakeLog) {
  const counted = readings.filter((r) => intakeOnThrough(intakeLog, r.at - COUNT_AFTER_MS, r.at));
  return counted.length === 0 ? null : counted.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.pa, 0) / counted.length;
}

// Days since the filter was changed (a fraction), never below 0.
export function daysInUse(room, now) {
  return room.filterChangedAt === null ? 0 : Math.max(0, (now - room.filterChangedAt) / DAY_MS);
}

// The `filter` input of alarms.evaluate: { sensor, pa (the counted pressure or null), cleanPa, finalPa, changeDays, daysInUse }.
export function filterInput(db, room, now) {
  const sensor = room.dpAddress !== null;
  const counted = sensor
    ? countedPa(db.filterReadingsWindow(room.id, now - MAX_AGE_MS, now + 1), db.machineLogWindow(room.id, 'intake', now - MAX_AGE_MS - COUNT_AFTER_MS, now), now)
    : null;
  return {
    sensor, pa: counted?.pa ?? null, cleanPa: room.filterCleanPa, finalPa: room.filterFinalPa,
    changeDays: room.filterChangeDays, daysInUse: daysInUse(room, now),
  };
}

// **Filter changed**: the change time, a change row with the last counted pressure of the past day (null without one), and
// the filter alarms cleared. Returns the room, or null when there is no such room. The caller drops the alarms' delay counts.
export function changeFilter(db, roomId, at) {
  if (!db.getRoom(roomId)) return null;
  const readings = db.filterReadingsWindow(roomId, at - DAY_MS, at + 1);
  const counted = countedPa(readings, db.machineLogWindow(roomId, 'intake', at - DAY_MS - COUNT_AFTER_MS, at), at, Infinity);
  db.markFilterChanged(roomId, at, counted?.pa ?? null);
  for (const key of FILTER_KEYS) db.clearAlarm(roomId, key, at);
  return db.getRoom(roomId);
}
```

### Task B5: The filter alarms and the controller's filter input

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/alarms-filter.test.js`
- Modify: `server/alarms.js`
- Modify: `server/controller.js`

`evaluate` takes `filter` (missing = no filter alarms, so older callers and tests are unchanged). Without a sensor the
pressure alarms clear; with one and no counted reading they pause (count dropped, an active alarm stays). `alarms.reset`
drops delay counts; the controller passes `filterInput(db, room, at)` and exposes `filterChanged(roomId)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `tests/alarms-filter.test.js`

```js
// The filter alarms (server/alarms.js, spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md section 5) and the controller's filter input.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { openDb } from '../server/db.js';
import { createAlarms } from '../server/alarms.js';
import { createController } from '../server/controller.js';
import { FILTER_KEYS } from '../server/filter.js';

const MIN = 60_000;
const bands = { co2Low: 600, co2High: 750, co2Safety: 1250, tLow: 21, tHigh: 23, rhLow: 85, rhHigh: 95, exchangeMaxMin: 10 };
const FILTER = { sensor: true, pa: 80, cleanPa: 40, finalPa: 150, changeDays: 30, daysInUse: 3 };

function setup(t) {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  t.after(() => db.close());
  const room = db.createRoom({ name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5 });
  let clock = 1_000_000;
  const alarms = createAlarms({ db, now: () => clock });
  return {
    db, alarms,
    tick(ms, filter) {
      clock += ms;
      alarms.evaluate(room, {
        readings: { t: 22, rh: 90, co2: 650 }, bands, faults: { trh: null, co2: null }, cappedInRow: 0, machines: null,
        filter: { ...FILTER, ...filter },
      });
    },
    active: () => db.activeAlarms(room.id).map((a) => [a.key, a.level, a.text]),
    room,
  };
}

test('no filter alarm for a filter in use 3 days at 80 Pa', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) s.tick(MIN);
  assert.deepEqual(s.active(), []);
});

test('filterDue: at once when the days in use reach the interval; not with no reminder; cleared when not due', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  s.tick(0, { daysInUse: 29.99 });
  assert.deepEqual(s.active(), []);
  s.tick(0, { daysInUse: 32.4 });
  assert.deepEqual(s.active(), [['filterDue', 'warning', 'Filter in use 32 days, change every 30']]);
  s.tick(MIN, { daysInUse: 32.5, changeDays: 40 }); // the interval made longer
  assert.deepEqual(s.active(), []);
  s.tick(MIN, { daysInUse: 400, changeDays: 0 });
  assert.deepEqual(s.active(), []);
});

test('filterDirty: at the change pressure for 10 min; clears below change pressure − 10', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  s.tick(0, { pa: 150 });
  s.tick(9 * MIN, { pa: 162 });
  assert.deepEqual(s.active(), []);
  s.tick(MIN, { pa: 162 });
  assert.deepEqual(s.active(), [['filterDirty', 'warning', 'Filter pressure 162 Pa, at or above the change pressure 150']]);
  s.tick(MIN, { pa: 141 });
  assert.equal(s.active().length, 1, 'inside the hysteresis');
  s.tick(MIN, { pa: 139.9 });
  assert.deepEqual(s.active(), []);
});

test('filterMissing: below half the clean pressure for 10 min; clears at 0.6 × clean', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  s.tick(0, { pa: 12 });
  s.tick(10 * MIN, { pa: 12 });
  assert.deepEqual(s.active(), [['filterMissing', 'warning', 'Filter pressure 12 Pa: filter missing or torn, or the sensor tube is off']]);
  s.tick(MIN, { pa: 23 });
  assert.equal(s.active().length, 1);
  s.tick(MIN, { pa: 24 });
  assert.deepEqual(s.active(), []);
});

test('no counted reading pauses the pressure alarms: an active one stays, a running count starts again', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  s.tick(0, { pa: 200 });
  s.tick(10 * MIN, { pa: 200 });
  s.tick(MIN, { pa: null });
  assert.equal(s.active().length, 1, 'the dirty alarm stays');
  const s2 = setup(t);
  s2.tick(0, { pa: 10 });
  s2.tick(9 * MIN, { pa: 10 });
  s2.tick(MIN, { pa: null });
  s2.tick(MIN, { pa: 10 });
  s2.tick(9 * MIN, { pa: 10 });
  assert.deepEqual(s2.active(), [], 'the missing count started again after the gap');
  s2.tick(MIN, { pa: 10 });
  assert.deepEqual(s2.active().map((a) => a[0]), ['filterMissing']);
});

test('without a sensor the pressure alarms clear; the reminder still works', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  s.tick(0, { pa: 200 });
  s.tick(10 * MIN, { pa: 200 });
  s.tick(MIN, { sensor: false, pa: null, daysInUse: 31 });
  assert.deepEqual(s.active().map((a) => a[0]), ['filterDue']);
});

test('reset drops the delay counts of the given keys', (t) => {
  const s = setup(t);
  s.tick(0, { pa: 200 });
  s.tick(9 * MIN, { pa: 200 });
  s.alarms.reset(s.room.id, FILTER_KEYS);
  s.tick(MIN, { pa: 200 });
  assert.deepEqual(s.active(), [], 'the count started again');
  s.tick(10 * MIN, { pa: 200 });
  assert.equal(s.active().length, 1);
});

test('an evaluate without filter input raises no filter alarm (an older caller)', (t) => {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  t.after(() => db.close());
  const room = db.createRoom({ name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5 });
  createAlarms({ db, now: () => 0 }).evaluate(room, {
    readings: { t: 22, rh: 90, co2: 650 }, bands, faults: { trh: null, co2: null }, cappedInRow: 0, machines: null,
  });
  assert.deepEqual(db.activeAlarms(room.id), []);
});

test('the controller passes the room\'s filter: a dirty filter with the intake on raises filterDirty after 10 min', async (t) => {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  t.after(() => db.close());
  let clock = 0;
  const id = db.createRoom({ name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5, dpAddress: 7 }, 0).id;
  db.logMachine(id, 'intake', { on: true }, 'manual', 0);
  const machines = { get: () => null, async command() {}, forget() {} };
  const controller = createController({ db, machines, now: () => clock });
  for (let i = 0; i <= 11; i++) {
    clock = 2 * MIN + i * MIN;
    db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: clock, ok: true, pa: 170 });
    await controller.run(clock);
  }
  assert.deepEqual(db.activeAlarms(id).map((a) => a.key), ['filterDirty']);
  controller.filterChanged(id); // the delay counts go; the API clears the alarm itself
});
```

### Task B6: API: Filter changed, the changes, the sensor in /sensors

**Files:**

`POST /api/rooms/:id/filter/changed` → 200 with the room (works without a controller); `GET /api/rooms/:id/filter/changes`
→ `[{ at, paBefore }]`; `/sensors` gains the fourth element; `dpAddress` joins `ADDRESS_FIELDS` so it is kept apart from the

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `tests/api-filter.test.js`

```js
// The filter over HTTP (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md sections 3 to 5): the room's filter fields, the filter pressure
// sensor in /sensors, Filter changed and the list of changes.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { openDb } from '../server/db.js';
import { createApp } from '../server/app.js';

const ROOM = { name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5 };

async function app(t, { controller = null } = {}) {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  const server = createApp(db, { controller });
  await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
  t.after(async () => { await new Promise((r) => server.close(r)); db.close(); });
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  const call = async (method, path, body) => {
    const res = await fetch(base + path, { method, headers: body ? { 'content-type': 'application/json' } : {}, body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined });
    const text = await res.text();
    return { status: res.status, body: text ? JSON.parse(text) : null };
  };
  return { db, call };
}

test('a room is made and changed with its filter settings and sensor address; an older page keeps them', async (t) => {
  const { call } = await app(t);
  const made = await call('POST', '/api/rooms', { ...ROOM, fanMaxPa: 250, filterCleanPa: 30, filterFinalPa: 120, filterChangeDays: 21, dpAddress: 9 });
  assert.equal(made.status, 201);
  assert.deepEqual([made.body.fanMaxPa, made.body.filterCleanPa, made.body.filterFinalPa, made.body.filterChangeDays, made.body.dpAddress], [250, 30, 120, 21, 9]);
  assert.equal(typeof made.body.filterChangedAt, 'number');
  const older = await call('PUT', `/api/rooms/${made.body.id}`, { ...ROOM, dpAddress: 9 }); // a page without the filter fields
  assert.deepEqual([older.body.fanMaxPa, older.body.filterChangeDays], [250, 21]);
  const bad = await call('PUT', `/api/rooms/${made.body.id}`, { ...ROOM, fanMaxPa: 20, filterCleanPa: 30 });
  assert.deepEqual(bad, { status: 400, body: { error: "Fan maximum pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop", field: 'fanMaxPa' } });
});

test('the filter sensor address must not be the outdoor sensor\'s, nor the outdoor sensor a room\'s filter sensor', async (t) => {
  const { db, call } = await app(t);
  db.setSetting('outdoorAddress', '20');
  assert.deepEqual(await call('POST', '/api/rooms', { ...ROOM, dpAddress: 20 }), {
    status: 400, body: { error: 'Address 20 is used by the outdoor sensor', field: 'dpAddress' },
  });
  await call('POST', '/api/rooms', { ...ROOM, dpAddress: 21 });
  const r = await call('PUT', '/api/settings', { outdoorAddress: 21 });
  assert.deepEqual(r, { status: 400, body: { error: 'Address 21 is used by Room 1', field: 'outdoorAddress' } });
});

test('/sensors gives the filter pressure sensor fourth, after the energy meter', async (t) => {
  const { db, call } = await app(t);
  const id = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, dpAddress: 7 }).id;
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: 100, ok: true, pa: 61.5 });
  const { body } = await call('GET', `/api/rooms/${id}/sensors`);
  assert.deepEqual(body.map((s) => s.kind), ['trh', 'co2', 'meter', 'dp']);
  assert.deepEqual(body[3], { kind: 'dp', address: 7, status: 'online', failuresInRow: 0, lastOk: { at: 100, pa: 61.5 }, readings: [{ at: 100, ok: true, pa: 61.5 }] });
});

test('Filter changed: the change time, a change row, the filter alarms cleared, the controller told; the list of changes', async (t) => {
  const told = [];
  const controller = { filterChanged: (id) => told.push(id), forget() {}, get: () => null };
  const { db, call } = await app(t, { controller });
  const id = db.createRoom(ROOM, 0).id;
  db.raiseAlarm(id, 'filterDue', 'warning', 'Filter in use 31 days, change every 30', 1000);
  const before = Date.now();
  const r = await call('POST', `/api/rooms/${id}/filter/changed`);
  assert.equal(r.status, 200);
  assert.ok(r.body.filterChangedAt >= before);
  assert.deepEqual(db.activeAlarms(id), []);
  assert.deepEqual(told, [id]);
  const list = await call('GET', `/api/rooms/${id}/filter/changes`);
  assert.deepEqual(list, { status: 200, body: [{ at: r.body.filterChangedAt, paBefore: null }] });
});

test('the filter routes: 404 for no room, other methods not found', async (t) => {
  const { db, call } = await app(t);
  assert.deepEqual(await call('POST', '/api/rooms/99/filter/changed'), { status: 404, body: { error: 'Room not found' } });
  assert.deepEqual(await call('GET', '/api/rooms/99/filter/changes'), { status: 404, body: { error: 'Room not found' } });
  const id = db.createRoom(ROOM).id;
  assert.equal((await call('GET', `/api/rooms/${id}/filter/changed`)).status, 404);
  assert.equal((await call('POST', `/api/rooms/${id}/filter/changes`)).status, 404);
  assert.equal((await call('POST', `/api/rooms/${id}/filter/changed`)).status, 200, 'works without a controller');
});
```

### Task B7: Usage: the intake estimate follows the filter

**Files:**

`estimateDay(ms, room, intakeFactor = 1)` with the factor from `fanWattFactor` (task A1). The day's work adds `pa`, the
mean counted pressure of the day (null without a sensor or a counted reading), cached with the run times once the day is
settled; `addFilterReading` (task B2) already drops a worked-out day that a late reading falls into.
