```js
// The intake fan's estimate against its filter (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 6): with a filter pressure sensor the
// day's mean counted pressure raises the intake's watts (sim-power.js fanWattFactor); without one, the rating as before.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { openDb } from '../server/db.js';
import { fixedZone, roomUsage, estimateDay } from '../server/usage.js';

const zone = fixedZone(480);
const H = 3_600_000;
const at = (day, hour, minute = 0) => Date.UTC(2026, 9, day, hour - 8, minute); // local time, UTC+8
const NOW = at(20, 10);
const PRICES = { electricity: 0.5, water: null };
const fields = { name: 'A', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5, intakeW: 100, exhaustW: 100 };

function setup(t, room = {}) {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  t.after(() => db.close());
  return { db, room: db.createRoom({ ...fields, ...room }, at(1, 0)) };
}
const log = (db, room, machine, time, on = true) => db.logMachine(room.id, machine, { on }, 'auto', time);

test('estimateDay: the intake times the factor, the other machines as rated', () => {
  const ms = { intake: { on: H }, exhaust: { on: H } };
  const est = estimateDay(ms, { ...fields, circulationW: 0, humidifierW: 0, dehumidifierW: 0, acInputW: 0 }, 1.075);
  assert.equal(est.intake, 0.1075);
  assert.equal(est.exhaust, 0.1);
  assert.equal(estimateDay(ms, { ...fields, circulationW: 0, humidifierW: 0, dehumidifierW: 0, acInputW: 0 }).intake, 0.1);
});

test('a day with counted pressure: intake kWh × fanWattFactor(mean Pa, clean, max)', (t) => {
  const { db, room } = setup(t, { dpAddress: 7 });
  log(db, room, 'intake', at(19, 0));
  log(db, room, 'exhaust', at(19, 0));
  log(db, room, 'intake', at(19, 2), false);
  log(db, room, 'exhaust', at(19, 2), false);
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: room.id, address: 7, at: at(19, 0, 0) + 30_000, ok: true, pa: 2 }); // spin-up: not counted
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: room.id, address: 7, at: at(19, 1), ok: true, pa: 110 });
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: room.id, address: 7, at: at(19, 1, 30), ok: true, pa: 130 });
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: room.id, address: 7, at: at(19, 3), ok: true, pa: 1 }); // intake off: not counted
  const day = roomUsage(db, room.id, { days: 2, prices: PRICES, now: NOW, zone }).days[0];
  // mean 120 Pa: 1 + 0.15 × (120 − 40) / (200 − 40) = 1.075; 2 h × 100 W = 0.2 kWh
  assert.equal(day.byMachine.intake, 0.215);
  assert.equal(day.byMachine.exhaust, 0.2);
  assert.equal(day.kWh, 0.415);
});

test('without a sensor, or a day without a counted reading, the intake is at its rating', (t) => {
  const { db, room } = setup(t);
  log(db, room, 'intake', at(19, 0));
  log(db, room, 'intake', at(19, 2), false);
  assert.equal(roomUsage(db, room.id, { days: 2, prices: PRICES, now: NOW, zone }).days[0].byMachine.intake, 0.2);
  const s = setup(t, { dpAddress: 7 });
  log(s.db, s.room, 'intake', at(19, 0));
  log(s.db, s.room, 'intake', at(19, 2), false);
  assert.equal(roomUsage(s.db, s.room.id, { days: 2, prices: PRICES, now: NOW, zone }).days[0].byMachine.intake, 0.2);
});

test('a reading written into a worked-out day makes it count again', (t) => {
  const { db, room } = setup(t, { dpAddress: 7 });
  log(db, room, 'intake', at(18, 0));
  log(db, room, 'intake', at(18, 2), false);
  const first = roomUsage(db, room.id, { days: 3, prices: PRICES, now: NOW, zone }).days[0];
  assert.equal(first.byMachine.intake, 0.2);
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: room.id, address: 7, at: at(18, 1), ok: true, pa: 200 }); // 1.15
  assert.equal(roomUsage(db, room.id, { days: 3, prices: PRICES, now: NOW, zone }).days[0].byMachine.intake, 0.23);
});
```

(branch `worktree-agent-a58413333a00ba2d8`, f42249b to 5351deb).

  them by hand or with `git apply` (save the block to a file first).

### Task C1: Simulation runs: the filter (settings, changes, watts, summary)

**Files:**
- Modify: `server/simulate.js`
- Modify: `server/simulations.js`
- Create: `tests/simulate-filter.test.js`
- Modify: `tests/simulate.test.js`

`server/simulations.js` checks the filter fields of a run (none given: no filter); `server/simulate.js` loads the filter as if it had passed `fanFlow` for its days in use, changes it on the reminder or, with a sensor, after 10 min of intake running at or above the change pressure, prices the intake by `fanWattFactor` and adds `summary.filter`. Suggest machines passes no filter fields (a test proves its runs have none). The existing summary-keys test gains `filter`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/simulate-filter.test.js`:

```js
// The intake filter in simulation runs (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 8): the run's filter settings, its changes by days
// and by pressure, the intake's watts against it and the filter part of the summary. No filter settings: no filter.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { simulate } from '../server/simulate.js';
import { validateSimulation, validateSizing } from '../server/simulations.js';
import { runSizing } from '../server/sizing.js';
import { DEFAULTS } from '../server/controller.js';

const physics = { wallUA: 80, outdoorHigh: 33, outdoorLow: 25, outdoorDew: 24, sunW: 300, fanFlow: 600, acBTU: 12000, humidifierLH: 2 };
const filter = { fanMaxPa: 200, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 150, filterLifeDays: 30, filterChangeDays: 0, filterUsedDays: 0, filterSensor: false };
const input = (over = {}, days = 1) => ({ physics: { ...physics, ...over }, crops: [{ crop: 'oyster', blocks: 500 }], farmType: 'ready', stage: 'fruiting', days });
const ctx = {
  volume: 60,
  ratings: { intakeW: 60, exhaustW: 60, circulationW: 60, humidifierW: 80, humidifierLH: 2, acInputW: 1050 },
  control: { ...DEFAULTS },
  program: null,
  prices: { electricity: null, water: null },
};

test('no filter settings: no filter in the summary', () => {
  assert.equal(simulate(input(), ctx).summary.filter, null);
});

test('a clean filter that never loads much: no changes, the pressure near clean, almost no unfiltered air', () => {
  const f = simulate(input({ ...filter, filterLifeDays: 365 }), ctx).summary.filter;
  assert.deepEqual(f.changes, []);
  assert.ok(f.meanFilterPa >= 40 && f.meanFilterPa < 42, `mean ${f.meanFilterPa} Pa`);
  assert.ok(f.unfilteredPct < 0.5, `unfiltered ${f.unfilteredPct} %`);
  assert.ok(f.meanExchangeM3h > 0);
});

test('a filter already at its change pressure: higher pressure, less air, a share through the leaks', () => {
  const clean = simulate(input({ ...filter, filterLifeDays: 365 }), ctx).summary.filter;
  const dirty = simulate(input({ ...filter, filterUsedDays: 30 }), ctx).summary.filter;
  assert.ok(dirty.meanFilterPa > 95, `mean ${dirty.meanFilterPa} Pa`);
  // (1 − f) ÷ (1 + f) with f ≈ 0.65: about 21 % of the air of an exchange
  assert.ok(dirty.unfilteredPct > 18 && dirty.unfilteredPct < 24, `unfiltered ${dirty.unfilteredPct} %`);
  assert.ok(dirty.meanExchangeM3h < clean.meanExchangeM3h);
});

test('changed every day: a change at the start of each day after the first, s from the start', () => {
  const f = simulate(input({ ...filter, filterLifeDays: 1, filterChangeDays: 1 }, 3), ctx).summary.filter;
  assert.deepEqual(f.changes, [86400, 172800]);
});

test('a filter in use as long as its interval at the start is changed at once', () => {
  const f = simulate(input({ ...filter, filterChangeDays: 2, filterUsedDays: 2 }, 3), ctx).summary.filter;
  assert.deepEqual(f.changes, [0, 172800]);
});

test('with a sensor the filter is changed after 10 min of intake running at or above the change pressure', () => {
  // The sensor reads the drop at the flow the fan still moves (filterPa = resistance × share): with the defaults it reaches
  // the change pressure 150 Pa at a loading of 4. A filter 5 life-days in use starts above it.
  const run = simulate(input({ ...filter, filterLifeDays: 1, filterUsedDays: 5, filterSensor: true }, 3), ctx);
  const f = run.summary.filter;
  assert.ok(f.changes.length >= 1, 'changed at least once');
  // the intake ran at least 10 min between the start and the first change
  const onBefore = run.machines.intake.reduce((a, sp) => a + Math.max(0, Math.min(sp.to, f.changes[0]) - sp.from), 0);
  assert.ok(onBefore >= 600, `intake on ${onBefore} s before the change`);
  assert.equal(f.changes.length, 1, 'clean after the change: not again within the run');
  assert.deepEqual(simulate(input({ ...filter, filterLifeDays: 1, filterUsedDays: 5 }, 3), ctx).summary.filter.changes, [], 'no sensor, no reminder: never changed');
});

test('the intake costs more against a dirty filter; the other fans do not', () => {
  const clean = simulate(input({ ...filter, filterLifeDays: 365 }), ctx).summary.energy.byMachine;
  const dirty = simulate(input({ ...filter, filterUsedDays: 60 }), ctx).summary.energy.byMachine;
  const hoursClean = clean.intake / 0.06, hoursDirty = dirty.intake / 0.06;
  assert.ok(hoursDirty > hoursClean * 1.02, `intake kWh ${clean.intake} → ${dirty.intake}`);
  assert.ok(dirty.exhaust >= clean.exhaust * 0.9);
});

test('validation: no filter field is fine; with any of them the four settings are needed and checked like the device app', () => {
  const v = (over) => validateSimulation(input(over));
  assert.equal(v({}).input.physics.fanMaxPa, undefined);
  const ok = v({ fanMaxPa: 200, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 150, filterLifeDays: 30 }).input.physics;
  assert.deepEqual([ok.filterChangeDays, ok.filterUsedDays, ok.filterSensor], [0, 0, false]);
  assert.deepEqual(v({ ...filter, filterSensor: true }).input.physics.filterSensor, true);
  assert.deepEqual(v({ filterSensor: true }), { error: 'Fan maximum pressure must be a whole number from 20 to 2000 Pa', field: 'physics.fanMaxPa' });
  assert.equal(v({ ...filter, filterLifeDays: 0 }).field, 'physics.filterLifeDays');
  assert.deepEqual(v({ ...filter, filterChangeDays: 400 }), { error: 'Filter change every must be a whole number from 0 to 365 days', field: 'physics.filterChangeDays' });
  assert.deepEqual(v({ ...filter, filterUsedDays: 1.5 }), { error: 'Filter in use must be a whole number from 0 to 365 days', field: 'physics.filterUsedDays' });
  assert.deepEqual(v({ ...filter, filterSensor: 'yes' }), { error: 'Filter pressure sensor must be true or false', field: 'physics.filterSensor' });
  assert.deepEqual(v({ ...filter, fanMaxPa: 40 }), { error: "Fan maximum pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop", field: 'physics.fanMaxPa' });
  assert.deepEqual(v({ ...filter, filterFinalPa: 30 }), { error: "Filter change pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop", field: 'physics.filterFinalPa' });
});

test('suggest machines runs with no filter: its physics carry no filter field, even when the request had them', () => {
  const checked = validateSizing({ ...input({ ...filter }), target: 90 });
  assert.equal(checked.input.physics.fanMaxPa, undefined);
  const seen = [];
  const fake = (inp) => {
    seen.push(inp.physics);
    return { summary: { inBand: { t: 99, rh: 99, co2: 99, all: 99 }, energy: { kWh: 1 }, water: { litres: 1, removedLitres: 0 } } };
  };
  runSizing(checked.input, ctx, { simulate: fake, list: [{ machines: { fanFlow: 150, humidifierLH: 1, dehumidifierLday: 0, acBTU: 9000 }, exchangeMaxMin: 10 }], days: 1 });
  assert.equal(seen.length, 1);
  for (const key of Object.keys(filter)) assert.equal(seen[0][key], undefined, key);
});
```

```diff
+// The intake filter of a run (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 8): the four settings of the device app's PARAMS, then the
+// run's own three. None of the seven given: the run has no filter.
+const FILTER_PHYSICS = ['fanMaxPa', 'filterCleanPa', 'filterFinalPa', 'filterLifeDays'];
+const FILTER_RUN = [
+  { field: 'filterChangeDays', label: 'Filter change every' },
+  { field: 'filterUsedDays', label: 'Filter in use' },
+];
 const RATING_FIELDS = ['intakeW', 'exhaustW', 'circulationW', 'humidifierW', 'humidifierLH', 'acInputW', 'dehumidifierW', 'dehumidifierLday'];
 const CONTROL_FIELDS = ['exchangeMaxMin', 'acDrop', 'exchangeEveryMin', 'humidifierDuringExchange', 'acFan'];

   }
   const clash = outdoorProblem(clean);
   if (clash) return { error: clash.message, field: `physics.${clash.field}` };
+  const bad = checkFilter(physics, clean);
+  if (bad) return bad;
   const badCrops = validateCrops(crops);

   return { input };

+// The filter fields of a run's physics into `clean`. → { error, field } or null.
+function checkFilter(physics, clean) {
+  if (![...FILTER_PHYSICS, ...FILTER_RUN.map((f) => f.field), 'filterSensor'].some((f) => physics[f] !== undefined)) return null;
+  for (const field of FILTER_PHYSICS) {
+    const { min, max, label, unit } = PARAMS[field];
+    const v = physics[field];
+    if (!Number.isInteger(v) || v < min || v > max) return { error: `${label} must be a whole number from ${min} to ${max} ${unit}`, field: `physics.${field}` };
+    clean[field] = v;
+  }
+  for (const { field, label } of FILTER_RUN) {
+    const v = physics[field] ?? 0;
+    if (!Number.isInteger(v) || v < 0 || v > 365) return { error: `${label} must be a whole number from 0 to 365 days`, field: `physics.${field}` };
+    clean[field] = v;
+  }
+  const sensor = physics.filterSensor ?? false;
+  if (typeof sensor !== 'boolean') return { error: 'Filter pressure sensor must be true or false', field: 'physics.filterSensor' };
+  clean.filterSensor = sensor;
+  const clash = filterProblem(clean);
+  return clash ? { error: clash.message, field: `physics.${clash.field}` } : null;
+}
+
 // Checks a sizing request { physics (walls, weather, sun), crops, farmType, stage, target }. → { input } or { error, field }.
 export function validateSizing(body) {
   if (body === null || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) return { error: 'Body must be a JSON object' };
```

### Task C2: Simulate stepper: the filter fields

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/web-simulate-filter.test.js`
- Modify: `tests/web-simulate-math.test.js`
- Modify: `web/simulate-dialog.js`
- Modify: `web/simulate-math.js`

`web/simulate-math.js` gains six filter fields (four of them compared with the device app's PARAMS by the existing test), prefilled from the last run, else the room, else the default; `filterUsedDays` is always the room's whole days since its last change; `filterSensor` follows the room's `dpAddress`. Step 1 checks the two cross checks with the server's words; the body sends `filterSensor`; the summary gets a Filter line. `web/simulate-dialog.js` shows an **Intake filter** fieldset.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/web-simulate-filter.test.js`:

```js
// The stepper's filter settings (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 8): prefilled from the room, checked like the server, sent
// with the run and said in the summary.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { initialState, checkStep, buildBody, summaryLines, firstField } from '../web/simulate-math.js';

const DAY = 86_400_000;
const now = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 22, 12);
const room = {
  id: 3, name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 3, volume: 72, humidifierLH: 2,
  fanMaxPa: 250, filterCleanPa: 30, filterFinalPa: 120, filterChangeDays: 21, filterChangedAt: now - 9.6 * DAY, dpAddress: 9,
};

test('the filter fields come from the room: its settings, the whole days since its last change, its pressure sensor', () => {
  const s = initialState({ room, now });
  assert.deepEqual(
    ['fanMaxPa', 'filterCleanPa', 'filterFinalPa', 'filterLifeDays', 'filterChangeDays', 'filterUsedDays'].map((k) => s.physics[k]),
    ['250', '30', '120', '30', '21', '9'],
  );
  assert.equal(s.filterSensor, true);
  const plain = initialState({ room: { id: 1, name: 'R', length: 2, width: 2, height: 2, volume: 8 }, now });
  assert.deepEqual(
    ['fanMaxPa', 'filterCleanPa', 'filterFinalPa', 'filterLifeDays', 'filterChangeDays', 'filterUsedDays'].map((k) => plain.physics[k]),
    ['200', '40', '150', '30', '30', '0'],
  );
  assert.equal(plain.filterSensor, false);
});

test("a last run gives its filter settings, but the days in use are the room's now", () => {
  const lastRun = { input: { physics: { fanMaxPa: 300, filterCleanPa: 50, filterFinalPa: 200, filterLifeDays: 45, filterChangeDays: 14, filterUsedDays: 2 } } };
  const s = initialState({ room, lastRun, now });
  assert.deepEqual(
    ['fanMaxPa', 'filterCleanPa', 'filterFinalPa', 'filterLifeDays', 'filterChangeDays', 'filterUsedDays'].map((k) => s.physics[k]),
    ['300', '50', '200', '45', '14', '9'],
  );
});

test('step 1 checks the filter numbers and that the fan and the change pressure are above the clean drop', () => {
  const s = initialState({ room, now });
  assert.deepEqual(checkStep(1, s), {});
  s.physics.filterLifeDays = '0';
  s.physics.filterUsedDays = '2.5';
  let e = checkStep(1, s);
  assert.equal(e['physics.filterLifeDays'], 'Filter life must be a whole number from 1 to 365 days');
  assert.equal(e['physics.filterUsedDays'], 'Filter in use must be a whole number from 0 to 365 days');
  const t = initialState({ room, now });
  t.physics.fanMaxPa = '30';
  t.physics.filterFinalPa = '25';
  e = checkStep(1, t);
  assert.equal(e['physics.fanMaxPa'], "Fan maximum pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop");
  assert.equal(e['physics.filterFinalPa'], "Filter change pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop");
  assert.equal(firstField(e), 'physics.fanMaxPa');
});

test("the body sends the filter with the room's sensor", () => {
  const p = buildBody(initialState({ room, now })).physics;
  assert.deepEqual(
    [p.fanMaxPa, p.filterCleanPa, p.filterFinalPa, p.filterLifeDays, p.filterChangeDays, p.filterUsedDays, p.filterSensor],
    [250, 30, 120, 30, 21, 9, true],
  );
});

test('the summary says the filter in one line', () => {
  const line = (s) => summaryLines(s, room).find((l) => l.label === 'Filter').text;
  const s = initialState({ room, now });
  assert.equal(line(s), 'Clean 30 Pa, change at 120 Pa, fan maximum 250 Pa, life 30 days; 9 days in use; changed every 21 days or at the change pressure (pressure sensor)');
  s.physics.filterChangeDays = '0';
  s.filterSensor = false;
  assert.equal(line(s), 'Clean 30 Pa, change at 120 Pa, fan maximum 250 Pa, life 30 days; 9 days in use; never changed (no reminder, no pressure sensor)');
  s.filterSensor = true;
  assert.equal(line(s), 'Clean 30 Pa, change at 120 Pa, fan maximum 250 Pa, life 30 days; 9 days in use; changed at the change pressure (pressure sensor)');
});
```

Create `tests/web-simulation-filter.test.js`:

```js
// The filter part of a run's results page (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 8): the input line, the filter tiles and the
// filter changes on the intake row of the timeline. A run without a filter (older runs, suggest machines) shows none of it.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { inputLine, filterView, timelineRows } from '../web/simulation-math.js';

const physics = { wallUA: 80, outdoorHigh: 33, outdoorLow: 25, outdoorDew: 24, sunW: 300, fanFlow: 600, acBTU: 12000, humidifierLH: 2 };
const filter = { fanMaxPa: 200, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 150, filterLifeDays: 30, filterChangeDays: 30, filterUsedDays: 12, filterSensor: true };
const input = (over = {}) => ({ physics: { ...physics, ...over }, crops: [{ crop: 'oyster', blocks: 500 }], farmType: 'ready', stage: 'fruiting', days: 7 });

test('the input line names the filter when the run had one', () => {
  assert.ok(!/filter/.test(inputLine(input())));
  assert.match(inputLine(input(filter)), / · filter 40 Pa clean, change at 150 Pa, 12 days in use, changed every 30 days or at the change pressure$/);
  assert.match(inputLine(input({ ...filter, filterChangeDays: 0, filterSensor: false })), / · filter 40 Pa clean, change at 150 Pa, 12 days in use, never changed$/);
  assert.match(inputLine(input({ ...filter, filterSensor: false })), / · filter 40 Pa clean, change at 150 Pa, 12 days in use, changed every 30 days$/);
});

test('the filter tiles; none for a run without a filter', () => {
  assert.equal(filterView({ days: 7 }), null);
  assert.equal(filterView({ days: 7, filter: null }), null);
  const v = filterView({ days: 7, filter: { changes: [86400, 2 * 86400 + 3600 * 5 + 60 * 3], meanExchangeM3h: 123.44, meanFilterPa: 97.04, unfilteredPct: 20.96 } });
  assert.deepEqual(v.tiles, [
    { label: 'Filter changes', value: '2', line: 'day 2 00:00, day 3 05:03' },
    { label: 'Mean filter pressure', value: '97.0 Pa', line: 'while the intake ran' },
    { label: 'Unfiltered air', value: '21.0 %', line: 'of the exchanges, in through the leaks' },
    { label: 'Mean air exchange', value: '123 m³/h', line: 'over the whole run' },
  ]);
  const none = filterView({ days: 1, filter: { changes: [], meanExchangeM3h: 50, meanFilterPa: null, unfilteredPct: 0 } });
  assert.deepEqual(none.tiles.slice(0, 2), [
    { label: 'Filter changes', value: 'None', line: 'not changed in the run' },
    { label: 'Mean filter pressure', value: '—', line: 'the intake never ran' },
  ]);
});

test('the intake row of the timeline marks the filter changes', () => {
  const machines = { intake: [{ from: 0, to: 600 }], exhaust: [], circulation: [], humidifier: [], dehumidifier: [], ac: [] };
  const rows = timelineRows(machines, 0, input(filter), [86400]);
  assert.deepEqual(rows.find((r) => r.key === 'intake').marks, [{ at: 86400, text: 'Filter changed' }]);
  assert.deepEqual(timelineRows(machines, 0, input()).find((r) => r.key === 'intake').marks, []);
});
```

```js
// The room dialog's Filter section and the room page's Filter card (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 3 and 5): texts, checks
// like the server's, and the card's words.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { FILTER } from '../server/rooms.js';
import { FILTER_SETTINGS, filterTexts, parseFilter, dpAddressOf, filterCard } from '../web/filter-math.js';

const DAY = 86_400_000;
const now = new Date(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0).getTime();

test('the settings are those of the server (server/rooms.js FILTER)', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(FILTER_SETTINGS.map((f) => [f.name, f.def, f.min, f.max]), FILTER.map((f) => [f.field, f.default, f.min, f.max]));
});

test('texts: the room\'s, else the defaults', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(filterTexts(null), { fanMaxPa: '200', filterCleanPa: '40', filterFinalPa: '150', filterChangeDays: '30' });
  assert.deepEqual(filterTexts({ fanMaxPa: 300, filterCleanPa: 25, filterFinalPa: 120, filterChangeDays: 0 }),
    { fanMaxPa: '300', filterCleanPa: '25', filterFinalPa: '120', filterChangeDays: '0' });
});

test('parse: whole numbers in range, the fan and the change pressure above the clean drop, as the server says it', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(parseFilter(filterTexts(null)), { values: { fanMaxPa: 200, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 150, filterChangeDays: 30 } });
  assert.deepEqual(parseFilter({ fanMaxPa: '10', filterCleanPa: '40.5', filterFinalPa: '150', filterChangeDays: '366' }).errors, {
    fanMaxPa: 'Intake fan maximum pressure must be a whole number from 20 to 2000 Pa',
    filterCleanPa: 'Clean filter pressure drop must be a whole number from 5 to 1000 Pa',
    filterChangeDays: 'Change the filter every must be a whole number from 0 to 365 days',
  });
  assert.deepEqual(parseFilter({ fanMaxPa: '40', filterCleanPa: '40', filterFinalPa: '30', filterChangeDays: '' }).errors, {
    fanMaxPa: "Fan maximum pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop",
    filterFinalPa: "Filter change pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop",
    filterChangeDays: 'Change the filter every must be a whole number from 0 to 365 days',
  });
});

test('the pressure sensor address: blank is none, else 1 to 247', () => {
  assert.equal(dpAddressOf(''), null);
  assert.equal(dpAddressOf(' 12 '), 12);
  assert.ok(Number.isNaN(dpAddressOf('0')));
  assert.ok(Number.isNaN(dpAddressOf('2.5')));
});

const room = { fanMaxPa: 200, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 150, filterChangeDays: 30, filterChangedAt: now - 12.4 * DAY, dpAddress: 9 };
const sensor = (over = {}) => ({ kind: 'dp', address: 9, status: 'online', failuresInRow: 0, lastOk: { at: now - 5000, pa: 97.2 }, readings: [], ...over });

test('the card: pressure on the bar between clean and the change pressure, days in use and when it is due', () => {
  const c = filterCard({ room, sensor: sensor(), changes: [], now });
  assert.equal(c.pressure.text, '97.2 Pa');
  assert.equal(c.pressure.status, 'Online');
  assert.deepEqual([c.pressure.min, c.pressure.clean, c.pressure.final, c.pressure.max], [0, 40, 150, 200]);
  assert.equal(c.pressure.value, 97.2);
  assert.equal(c.pressure.over, false);
  assert.equal(c.age, '12 days in use');
  const due = new Date(room.filterChangedAt + 30 * DAY);
  assert.equal(c.due, `Change by ${due.getDate()} ${['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'][due.getMonth()]} (every 30 days)`);
  assert.equal(c.overdue, false);
  assert.equal(c.changes.length, 0);
});

test('the card without a sensor, without a reminder, overdue, at the change pressure, with past changes', () => {
  const plain = filterCard({ room: { ...room, dpAddress: null, filterChangeDays: 0 }, sensor: null, changes: [], now });
  assert.equal(plain.pressure, null);
  assert.equal(plain.noSensor, 'No pressure sensor');
  assert.equal(plain.due, 'No reminder');
  const late = filterCard({ room: { ...room, filterChangedAt: now - 33 * DAY }, sensor: sensor({ lastOk: { at: now - 5000, pa: 162 } }), changes: [], now });
  assert.equal(late.overdue, true);
  assert.match(late.due, /^Due since \d+ \w+ \(every 30 days\)$/);
  assert.equal(late.pressure.over, true);
  assert.equal(late.age, '33 days in use');
  const waiting = filterCard({ room, sensor: sensor({ status: 'no-data', lastOk: null }), changes: [], now });
  assert.equal(waiting.pressure.text, 'No reading yet');
  assert.equal(waiting.pressure.value, null);
  const changes = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => ({ at: now - (i + 1) * 30 * DAY, paBefore: i === 0 ? null : 140 + i }));
  const c = filterCard({ room, sensor: sensor(), changes, now });
  assert.equal(c.changes.length, 5);
  assert.match(c.changes[0], /^Changed \d+ \w+ \d\d:\d\d$/);
  assert.match(c.changes[1], /^Changed \d+ \w+ \d\d:\d\d at 141 Pa$/);
  assert.equal(filterCard({ room: { ...room, filterChangedAt: now - 0.4 * DAY }, sensor: null, changes: [], now }).age, 'Changed today');
  assert.equal(filterCard({ room: { ...room, filterChangedAt: now - 1.2 * DAY }, sensor: null, changes: [], now }).age, '1 day in use');
});

test('the room dialog has an input and an error line for the sensor address and each setting, and sends them', () => {
  const html = readFileSync(new URL('../web/index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
  for (const name of ['dpAddress', ...FILTER_SETTINGS.map((f) => f.name)]) {
    assert.match(html, new RegExp(`<input id="f-${name}" name="${name}"`), name);
    assert.match(html, new RegExp(`id="e-${name}"`), name);
  }
  const js = readFileSync(new URL('../web/room-dialog.js', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
  assert.match(js, /\.\.\.FILTER_NAMES/);
  assert.match(js, /dpAddress: dp,/);
  assert.match(js, /\.\.\.filter\.values,/);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `web/filter-math.js`:

```js
// Pure helpers for the room dialog's Filter section and the room page's Filter card (no DOM, so they can be tested in Node).
// Spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md sections 3 and 5.

import { meterAddressOf } from './ratings-math.js';

const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
const MONTHS = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
const STATUS_WORDS = { 'not-wired': 'Not wired', 'no-data': 'No data yet', online: 'Online', timeout: 'Timeout', offline: 'Offline' };
const finite = (v) => typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v);
const pad2 = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const dayMonth = (ms) => `${new Date(ms).getDate()} ${MONTHS[new Date(ms).getMonth()]}`;
const dateTime = (ms) => `${dayMonth(ms)} ${pad2(new Date(ms).getHours())}:${pad2(new Date(ms).getMinutes())}`;

// The four settings (server/rooms.js FILTER; a test compares them), whole numbers.
export const FILTER_SETTINGS = [
  { name: 'fanMaxPa', label: 'Intake fan maximum pressure', unit: 'Pa', def: 200, min: 20, max: 2000 },
  { name: 'filterCleanPa', label: 'Clean filter pressure drop', unit: 'Pa', def: 40, min: 5, max: 1000 },
  { name: 'filterFinalPa', label: 'Filter change pressure', unit: 'Pa', def: 150, min: 5, max: 2000 },
  { name: 'filterChangeDays', label: 'Change the filter every', unit: 'days', def: 30, min: 0, max: 365 },
];
export const FILTER_NAMES = FILTER_SETTINGS.map((f) => f.name);

// A room (or null, for a new room) → the text of each setting; a missing number falls back to the default.
export function filterTexts(room) {
  return Object.fromEntries(FILTER_SETTINGS.map((f) => [f.name, String(finite(room?.[f.name]) ? room[f.name] : f.def)]));
}

// The inputs' texts → { values } or { errors: { name: message } }, in the server's words. The dialog always sends all four:
// the server keeps a setting that is left out.
export function parseFilter(texts) {
  const values = {};
  const errors = {};
  for (const f of FILTER_SETTINGS) {
    const s = String(texts?.[f.name] ?? '').trim();
    const n = /^[0-9]{1,4}$/.test(s) ? Number(s) : NaN;
    if (n >= f.min && n <= f.max) values[f.name] = n;
    else errors[f.name] = `${f.label} must be a whole number from ${f.min} to ${f.max} ${f.unit}`;
  }
  if (!errors.filterCleanPa) {
    if (!errors.fanMaxPa && values.fanMaxPa <= values.filterCleanPa) errors.fanMaxPa = "Fan maximum pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop";
    if (!errors.filterFinalPa && values.filterFinalPa <= values.filterCleanPa) errors.filterFinalPa = "Filter change pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop";
  }
  return Object.keys(errors).length ? { errors } : { values };
}

// The filter pressure sensor address: blank = none, else a whole number 1 to 247; NaN if wrong.
export const dpAddressOf = meterAddressOf;

// The Filter card of the room page. room: GET /api/rooms/:id; sensor: the 'dp' element of the sensors reply (or null);
// changes: GET /api/rooms/:id/filter/changes. →
// { pressure: { text, status, value, min, clean, final, max, over } | null (no sensor), noSensor, age, due, overdue, changes: [text] }.
export function filterCard({ room, sensor, changes, now }) {
  const hasSensor = finite(room?.dpAddress) && sensor && sensor.status !== 'not-wired';
  let pressure = null;
  if (hasSensor) {
    const pa = finite(sensor.lastOk?.pa) ? sensor.lastOk.pa : null;
    pressure = {
      text: pa === null ? 'No reading yet' : `${pa.toFixed(1)} Pa`,
      status: STATUS_WORDS[sensor.status] ?? String(sensor.status),
      value: pa, min: 0, clean: room.filterCleanPa, final: room.filterFinalPa, max: Math.max(room.fanMaxPa, pa ?? 0),
      over: pa !== null && pa >= room.filterFinalPa,
    };
  }
  const since = finite(room?.filterChangedAt) ? room.filterChangedAt : now;
  const days = Math.max(0, Math.floor((now - since) / DAY_MS));
  const every = room?.filterChangeDays ?? 0;
  const dueAt = since + every * DAY_MS;
  const overdue = every > 0 && now >= dueAt;
  return {
    pressure,
    noSensor: 'No pressure sensor',
    age: (now - since) < DAY_MS ? 'Changed today' : `${days} ${days === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'} in use`,
    due: every > 0 ? `${overdue ? 'Due since' : 'Change by'} ${dayMonth(dueAt)} (every ${every} days)` : 'No reminder',
    overdue,
    changes: (Array.isArray(changes) ? changes : []).slice(0, 5)
      .map((c) => `Changed ${dateTime(c.at)}${finite(c.paBefore) ? ` at ${Math.round(c.paBefore)} Pa` : ''}`),
  };
}
```

### Task C5: Room page: the Intake filter card

**Files:**
- Modify: `tests/web-filter-math.test.js`
- Create: `web/filter-card.js`
- Modify: `web/room-detail.js`
- Modify: `web/styles.css`

New `web/filter-card.js` (`createFilterCard(host, { roomId, onChanged })` → `{ update(room, sensor), destroy() }`): the pressure on a `<meter>` (low = clean, high = change pressure), days in use and the due date, **Filter changed** with its own confirm `<dialog>`, the last 5 changes. `web/room-detail.js` keeps the `dp` sensor, makes the card, updates it each refresh, reloads the page after a change (the alarms clear) and destroys it with a missing room and on unmount. No `web/api.js` change: the card uses the generic `request`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `web/filter-card.js`:

```js
// The Filter card of the room page (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 5): the filter pressure now on a bar marked clean and
// change pressure (or "No pressure sensor"), the days in use and when it is due, a Filter changed button with a confirm and
// the last 5 changes. createFilterCard(host, { roomId, onChanged }) → { update(room, sensor), destroy() }.
// The changes are asked when the card is made and after a change; the room and the sensor come with the page's refresh.

import { request, OFFLINE } from './api.js';
import { filterCard } from './filter-math.js';

function el(tag, className, content) {
  const node = document.createElement(tag);
  if (className) node.className = className;
  if (content !== undefined) node.textContent = content;
  return node;
}

export function createFilterCard(host, { roomId, onChanged }) {
  let alive = true;
  let room = null;
  let sensor = null;
  let changes = [];
  let seq = 0; // every changes load takes a token; a reply that finds it changed is dropped

  host.className = 'card filter-card';
  host.hidden = true;
  const head = el('div', 'sim-card-head');
  const changed = el('button', '', 'Filter changed');
  changed.type = 'button';
  head.append(el('h2', '', 'Intake filter'), changed);
  const body = el('div', 'filter-body');
  const error = el('p', 'meta warn');
  error.setAttribute('role', 'alert');
  error.hidden = true;
  const list = el('ul', 'filter-changes');

  const confirm = el('dialog', 'confirm');
  confirm.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', `filter-confirm-${roomId}`);
  const form = el('form');
  form.method = 'dialog';
  const title = el('h2', '', 'Filter changed');
  title.id = `filter-confirm-${roomId}`;
  const actions = el('div', 'actions');
  const cancel = el('button', '', 'Cancel');
  cancel.value = 'cancel';
  const ok = el('button', 'primary', 'Filter changed');
  ok.value = 'changed';
  actions.append(cancel, ok);
  form.append(title, el('p', '', 'Record a new filter now? The days in use start again and the filter alarms clear.'), actions);
  confirm.append(form);
  host.append(head, body, error, list, confirm);

  function paint() {
    if (!room) return;
    const c = filterCard({ room, sensor, changes, now: Date.now() });
    const parts = [];
    if (c.pressure) {
      const p = c.pressure;
      if (p.value === null) parts.push(el('p', 'meta', p.text));
      else {
        const line = el('p', 'reading');
        const span = el('span');
        span.append(el('span', 'val', p.value.toFixed(1)), el('span', 'unit', 'Pa'));
        line.append(span);
        parts.push(line);
        const meter = el('meter', 'filter-meter');
        Object.assign(meter, { min: p.min, max: p.max, low: p.clean, high: p.final, optimum: p.min, value: p.value });
        meter.setAttribute('aria-label', `Filter pressure ${p.text}`);
        parts.push(meter);
      }
      parts.push(el('p', 'meta', `clean ${p.clean} Pa · change at ${p.final} Pa · ${p.status}`));
      if (p.over) parts.push(el('p', 'meta warn', 'At or above the change pressure: change the filter'));
    } else {
      parts.push(el('p', 'addr', c.noSensor));
    }
    parts.push(el('p', 'meta', c.age), el('p', c.overdue ? 'meta warn' : 'meta', c.due));
    body.replaceChildren(...parts);
    list.replaceChildren(...c.changes.map((t) => el('li', 'meta', t)));
    host.hidden = false;
  }

  async function loadChanges() {
    const me = ++seq;
    try {
      const data = await request('GET', `/api/rooms/${roomId}/filter/changes`);
      if (!alive || me !== seq) return;
      changes = Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
      paint();
    } catch {
      // the list stays as it was; the card itself still shows
    }
  }

  changed.addEventListener('click', () => {
    confirm.returnValue = '';
    confirm.showModal();
  });

  confirm.addEventListener('close', async () => {
    if (!alive || confirm.returnValue !== 'changed') return;
    error.hidden = true;
    changed.disabled = true;
    try {
      room = await request('POST', `/api/rooms/${roomId}/filter/changed`);
      if (!alive) return;
      paint();
      loadChanges();
      onChanged?.();
    } catch (err) {
      if (!alive) return;
      error.textContent = err.status === 0 ? `${OFFLINE}. Try again.` : err.message;
      error.hidden = false;
    } finally {
      changed.disabled = false;
    }
  });

  loadChanges();

  return {
    // room: GET /api/rooms/:id; s: the 'dp' element of the sensors reply, or null.
    update(r, s) {
      room = r;
      sensor = s ?? null;
      paint();
    },
    destroy() {
      alive = false;
      seq++;
      if (confirm.open) confirm.close();
      host.replaceChildren();
    },
  };
}
```

Change `README.md`:

```diff
diff --git a/README.md b/README.md
index 3f662e9..e7acbda 100644
--- a/README.md
+++ b/README.md
@@ -40,6 +40,8 @@ Machines work the same way: add a relay module and an aircon gateway on the devi
 
 **Dehumidifier.** A room can have a dehumidifier on output 5 of its relay module (Edit room → Power and water → "Dehumidifier fitted", with its power and its capacity in L/day as on its label). Auto switches it on at the upper humidity limit and off at the middle of the band, only once the humidifier has been off for 30 min (a room that needed the humidifier a moment ago is not wet by itself: an air exchange wets it for a few minutes) and never together with the humidifier; its compressor gets 3 min between changes. A fitted dehumidifier has a machine tile, a timeline row, alarms and a share of the energy; the Simulate stepper asks for its size (0 = none) and the results show the water it took out. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-dehumidifier-design.md`.
 
+**Intake filter.** Each room has a filter on its intake fan (Edit room → Intake filter: the fan's maximum pressure from its datasheet, the clean filter's pressure drop, the pressure at which it is changed, and how often to change it, 0 = no reminder). The room page's **Intake filter** card shows the days in use and the due date, and **Filter changed** records a new filter (the days start again, the filter alarms clear). With an optional filter pressure sensor (Edit room → Sensors → "Filter pressure sensor address") the card shows the pressure across the filter; readings count once the intake has run 60 s. Alarms: the filter due, the pressure at or above the change pressure for 10 min (dirty), and below half the clean drop for 10 min (missing or torn, or the sensor tube is off). With a sensor the intake fan's estimated energy rises with the pressure. In the simulator a filter loads with the air it passes and the fan moves less air as it does (the exhaust fan pulls the shortfall in through the leaks, unfiltered); the device app's sim card shows the filter and has a **Change filter** button (a Reset keeps the filter as it is). Simulation runs take the room's filter settings and its days in use, change it on the reminder (or at the change pressure with a sensor) and report the changes, the mean filter pressure and the unfiltered share. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md`.
+
 **Simulation runs.** On the Rooms list (or a room's Simulations card), **Simulate** asks in three steps for the room's walls, weather and machines (wall heat transfer, outdoor day high, night low and dew point, sun on the roof, fan flow, aircon size, humidifier output), the crops and the start stage, and how long (7 days, or the whole program). The run goes on in the background (a 68-day program takes a few seconds) with the room's own control settings, and its page shows the time in band (temperature, humidity, CO₂, all three; per stage), charts with the band as it moved and the outdoor air, the machine timeline, and the energy (kWh, RM, per machine and per day) and water (litres). The aircon energy comes from the size you enter (COP 3). The last 20 runs of each room are kept. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-20-crops-and-simulation-design.md`.
 
 The farm app polls every wired sensor and machine every reading interval (5 s by default; change it on the Settings page, 1–300 s). A sensor is **online** when the latest poll answered, **timeout** after 1–2 failed polls in a row, **offline** after 3 or more. Stop the device app to see what a dead gateway looks like.

 | Energy meter | function 04, input registers 0x000C–0x000D and 0x0156–0x0157 | active power W and total energy kWh, each a float32, high word first |
+| Filter pressure sensor | function 04, input register 0 | pressure across the filter, Pa × 10 (signed) |
```

```diff
 | Fan curve | a straight line from (0, `fanMaxPa`) through the clean operating point (`fanFlow`, `filterCleanPa`). The duct and grille are part of that clean point |
 | Filter resistance | `R` = `filterCleanPa` + (`filterFinalPa` − `filterCleanPa`) × `Lf`: the filter's pressure drop at `fanFlow`, in proportion to the flow at other flows |
 | Intake share | `f` = `fanMaxPa` ÷ (`fanMaxPa` − `filterCleanPa` + `R`): the intake flow ÷ `fanFlow` where the two lines meet (1 when clean; 0.65 at the final pressure with the defaults) |
-| Filter pressure | `R` × `f` while the intake fan runs, else 0 |
+| Filter pressure | `R` × `f` while the intake fan runs, else 0: the drop at the flow the fan still moves, which is what a sensor reads. It is below `R`, so a sensor reads `filterFinalPa` only past `Lf` = 1 (at `Lf` = 4 with the defaults, `f` ≈ 0.31) |
 | Air exchange | both fans: `fanFlow` × (1 + `f`) ÷ 2 (the exhaust fan keeps pulling; the shortfall comes in through the leaks, unfiltered); intake alone: `SINGLE_FAN` × `fanFlow` × `f`; exhaust alone: as now; never below the leakage, as now |
 | Unfiltered share | both fans: (1 − `f`) ÷ (1 + `f`) of the exchange; reported, not used by the physics |
 | Loading | each sub-step `Lf` += air through the filter (m³) ÷ (`filterLifeDays` × 24 × `fanFlow`). Air through the filter = `fanFlow` × `f` (both fans) or `SINGLE_FAN` × `fanFlow` × `f` (intake alone), × the step time. The filter loads with the air it passes, not with the days |

 | `filterFinalPa` ≤ `filterCleanPa` | "Filter change pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop", `filterFinalPa` |

 ## 4. Pressure sensor
 
 | Item | Rule |
 |---|---|
 | Device | an RS485 differential pressure transmitter across the filter: function 04, input register 0 = ΔP in Pa × 10, signed. *Default, to be checked against the owner's transmitter* |
-| Polling | after the room's T/RH and CO₂ sensors, before the energy meter; one read; a failed read = a failed poll |
-| Bad reading | outside −50 to 2000 Pa: stored with `ok` 0 and the error "Out of range" |
-| Status | as the other sensors: online, timeout (1–2 failed in a row), offline (3 or more), `no-data`, `not-set` |
-| Storage | table `filter_readings (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, room_id INTEGER NOT NULL, address INTEGER NOT NULL, at INTEGER NOT NULL, ok INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (ok IN (0,1)), pa REAL, error TEXT)`, index `(room_id, at)`; deleted with the room |
+| Polling | after the room's T/RH and CO₂ sensors, before the energy meter; one read; a failed read = a failed poll. The poller decodes the register; the Modbus client stays generic |
+| Bad reading | outside −50 to 2000 Pa: stored with `ok` 0, the value kept, and the error "Out of range" |
+| Status | as the energy meter: online, timeout (1–2 failed in a row), offline (3 or more), `no-data`, `not-wired` (no address) |
+| Storage | table `filter_readings (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, room_id INTEGER NOT NULL, address INTEGER NOT NULL, at INTEGER NOT NULL, ok INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (ok IN (0,1)), pa REAL, error TEXT)`, the energy meter readings' three indexes on `(room_id, address, …)`; deleted with the room |
 | Counts when | the intake fan has been commanded on for at least 60 s (from `machine_log`); a reading at other times is stored and shown but judged by nothing |
 | API | `GET /api/rooms/:id/sensors` returns a fourth element `{ kind: 'dp', address, status, failuresInRow, lastOk: { at, pa } \| null, readings }`, like the energy meter |
 
@@ -66,7 +66,7 @@ The room dialog gets a section **Filter** with the first four fields; `dpAddress
 
 | Item | Rule |
 |---|---|
-| **Filter changed** | `POST /api/rooms/:id/filter/changed` → sets `filterChangedAt` to now, adds a row to `filter_changes (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, room_id INTEGER NOT NULL, at INTEGER NOT NULL, pa_before REAL)` (`pa_before`: the last counted reading, or null), clears `filterDue`, `filterDirty` and `filterMissing`; returns the room. `GET /api/rooms/:id/filter/changes` → the last 20, newest first |
+| **Filter changed** | `POST /api/rooms/:id/filter/changed` → sets `filterChangedAt` to now, adds a row to `filter_changes (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, room_id INTEGER NOT NULL, at INTEGER NOT NULL, pa_before REAL)` (`pa_before`: the last counted reading of the past day, or null: the intake is usually off while the filter is changed), clears `filterDue`, `filterDirty` and `filterMissing`; returns the room. `GET /api/rooms/:id/filter/changes` → the last 20, newest first |
 | `filterDue` (warning, no delay) | `filterChangeDays` > 0 and days since `filterChangedAt` ≥ `filterChangeDays`. Text "Filter in use 32 days, change every 30". Clears on **Filter changed** or when the setting moves the due date past now |
 | `filterDirty` (warning, 10 min) | a sensor is set and the counted ΔP ≥ `filterFinalPa`. Text "Filter pressure 162 Pa, at or above the change pressure 150". Clears below `filterFinalPa` − 10 or on **Filter changed** |
 | `filterMissing` (warning, 10 min) | a sensor is set and the counted ΔP < `filterCleanPa` ÷ 2. Text "Filter pressure 12 Pa: filter missing or torn, or the sensor tube is off". Clears at or above 0.6 × `filterCleanPa` or on **Filter changed** |
@@ -87,17 +87,17 @@ The room dialog gets a section **Filter** with the first four fields; `dpAddress
 | Item | Rule |
 |---|---|
 | Sensor kind | `dp`, value `pa` (−50 to 2000), typed by hand, answering as section 4 |
-| Simulated room | parameters `fanMaxPa`, `filterCleanPa`, `filterFinalPa`, `filterLifeDays` with the section 3 defaults (old saved simulations get them); a seventh link `filterProbe` (a `dp` device) that each tick gets `filterPa`; the sim card shows the filter pressure and loading and a **Change filter** button (`changeFilter`) |
+| Simulated room | parameters `fanMaxPa`, `filterCleanPa`, `filterFinalPa`, `filterLifeDays` with the section 3 defaults (old saved simulations get them); a seventh link `filterProbe` (a `dp` device) that each tick gets `filterPa`; the sim card shows the filter pressure and loading and a **Change filter** button (`changeFilter`). A **Reset** puts the air back and keeps the filter as it is |
 | Two buttons | the device's **Change filter** changes the simulated filter; the farm's **Filter changed** records it. The owner presses both, as with a real filter |
 
 ## 8. Simulation runs
 
 | Item | Rule |
 |---|---|
-| New physics fields | `fanMaxPa`, `filterCleanPa`, `filterFinalPa`, `filterChangeDays` (prefilled from the room), `filterLifeDays` (default 30, 1–365), `filterUsedDays` (the filter's age at the start: prefilled with the room's days since `filterChangedAt`, 0–365), `filterSensor` (true when the room has `dpAddress`) |
+| New physics fields | `fanMaxPa`, `filterCleanPa`, `filterFinalPa`, `filterChangeDays` (prefilled from the room), `filterLifeDays` (default 30, 1–365), `filterUsedDays` (the filter's age at the start: prefilled with the room's whole days since `filterChangedAt`, 0–365), `filterSensor` (true when the room has `dpAddress`; not asked). As the other fields, the stepper prefills from the room's last run first, except `filterUsedDays`, always the room's now. Any filter field sent: the first four are required; `filterChangeDays` and `filterUsedDays` default to 0, `filterSensor` to false. None sent: no filter |
 | Start | `Lf` = `filterUsedDays` ÷ `filterLifeDays`, as if the filter had passed `fanFlow` all that time (a conservative start) |
-| Changes in the run | the filter is changed (`Lf` = 0) when `filterChangeDays` > 0 and that many days passed since the last change, or, with `filterSensor`, when `filterPa` stayed at or above `filterFinalPa` for 10 min with the intake running |
-| Results | adds the change times (marks on the timeline), the mean exchange flow, the mean filter pressure while the intake ran and the unfiltered share (% of the fresh air through the leaks) |
+| Changes in the run | at the start of a 10 s step, the filter is changed (`Lf` = 0) when `filterChangeDays` > 0 and that many days passed since the last change (before the run: `filterUsedDays` ago, so a filter in use that long is changed at once), or, with `filterSensor`, after 10 min of intake running at or above `filterFinalPa`, added up over the exchanges and started again when the intake runs below it |
+| Results | `summary.filter` = `{ changes (s from the start), meanExchangeM3h (over the whole run), meanFilterPa (while the intake ran; null if it never ran), unfilteredPct (of the exchange while the intake ran, the part through the leaks) }`, or null without a filter. The page shows them as an **Intake filter** card, the changes as marks on the intake row and the filter in the input line |
 | Old runs | runs saved before this iteration show no filter lines; a run without filter fields has no filter (`Lf` 0) |
 | Suggest machines | unchanged: it passes no filter fields, and a run without them keeps `Lf` at 0 (`f` = 1) |
 
```

The owner's decision ("What the sensor reads, 80 Pa"). `device/sim-model.js`: the sensor reading while the intake runs is `p = min(fanMaxPa, clean + (final − clean) × Lf)` and the intake share `f = max(0, (fanMaxPa − p) ÷ (fanMaxPa − clean))`; `filterOf` → `{ share, pressurePa }` (was `resistancePa`); `acting.filterPa = p` while the intake runs. Loading, exchange, unfiltered and watts rules are unchanged. The change pressure default goes from 150 to 80 Pa everywhere (FILTER_DEFAULTS, the device PARAMS, `server/rooms.js` FILTER, the version 14 column default, the pages) and a third check, `filterFinalPa` ≥ `fanMaxPa` → "Filter change pressure must be below the fan's maximum pressure" (field `filterFinalPa`), is made in the device app (`filterProblem`, so also the simulation runs), the room settings, the room dialog and the stepper. Help text in the dialog and the stepper; the spec (sections 2, 3, 9) and the README follow.

Change `README.md`:

```diff
diff --git a/README.md b/README.md
index e7acbda..b8e2e34 100644
--- a/README.md
+++ b/README.md
@@ -40,7 +40,7 @@ Machines work the same way: add a relay module and an aircon gateway on the devi
 
 **Dehumidifier.** A room can have a dehumidifier on output 5 of its relay module (Edit room → Power and water → "Dehumidifier fitted", with its power and its capacity in L/day as on its label). Auto switches it on at the upper humidity limit and off at the middle of the band, only once the humidifier has been off for 30 min (a room that needed the humidifier a moment ago is not wet by itself: an air exchange wets it for a few minutes) and never together with the humidifier; its compressor gets 3 min between changes. A fitted dehumidifier has a machine tile, a timeline row, alarms and a share of the energy; the Simulate stepper asks for its size (0 = none) and the results show the water it took out. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-dehumidifier-design.md`.
 
-**Intake filter.** Each room has a filter on its intake fan (Edit room → Intake filter: the fan's maximum pressure from its datasheet, the clean filter's pressure drop, the pressure at which it is changed, and how often to change it, 0 = no reminder). The room page's **Intake filter** card shows the days in use and the due date, and **Filter changed** records a new filter (the days start again, the filter alarms clear). With an optional filter pressure sensor (Edit room → Sensors → "Filter pressure sensor address") the card shows the pressure across the filter; readings count once the intake has run 60 s. Alarms: the filter due, the pressure at or above the change pressure for 10 min (dirty), and below half the clean drop for 10 min (missing or torn, or the sensor tube is off). With a sensor the intake fan's estimated energy rises with the pressure. In the simulator a filter loads with the air it passes and the fan moves less air as it does (the exhaust fan pulls the shortfall in through the leaks, unfiltered); the device app's sim card shows the filter and has a **Change filter** button (a Reset keeps the filter as it is). Simulation runs take the room's filter settings and its days in use, change it on the reminder (or at the change pressure with a sensor) and report the changes, the mean filter pressure and the unfiltered share. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md`.
+**Intake filter.** Each room has a filter on its intake fan (Edit room → Intake filter: the fan's maximum pressure from its datasheet, the clean filter's pressure drop, the change pressure: the filter pressure sensor's reading at which to change it with the intake running, 80 Pa by default and below the fan's maximum, and how often to change it, 0 = no reminder). The room page's **Intake filter** card shows the days in use and the due date, and **Filter changed** records a new filter (the days start again, the filter alarms clear). With an optional filter pressure sensor (Edit room → Sensors → "Filter pressure sensor address") the card shows the pressure across the filter; readings count once the intake has run 60 s. Alarms: the filter due, the pressure at or above the change pressure for 10 min (dirty), and below half the clean drop for 10 min (missing or torn, or the sensor tube is off). With a sensor the intake fan's estimated energy rises with the pressure. In the simulator a filter loads with the air it passes and the fan moves less air as it does (the exhaust fan pulls the shortfall in through the leaks, unfiltered); the device app's sim card shows the filter and has a **Change filter** button (a Reset keeps the filter as it is). Simulation runs take the room's filter settings and its days in use, change it on the reminder (or at the change pressure with a sensor) and report the changes, the mean filter pressure and the unfiltered share. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md`.
 
 **Simulation runs.** On the Rooms list (or a room's Simulations card), **Simulate** asks in three steps for the room's walls, weather and machines (wall heat transfer, outdoor day high, night low and dew point, sun on the roof, fan flow, aircon size, humidifier output), the crops and the start stage, and how long (7 days, or the whole program). The run goes on in the background (a 68-day program takes a few seconds) with the room's own control settings, and its page shows the time in band (temperature, humidity, CO₂, all three; per stage), charts with the band as it moved and the outdoor air, the machine timeline, and the energy (kWh, RM, per machine and per day) and water (litres). The aircon energy comes from the size you enter (COP 3). The last 20 runs of each room are kept. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-20-crops-and-simulation-design.md`.
 
```

```diff
 const DEHUM_MAX = 1.2;
 const dehumShare = (W) => clamp((W - satW(DEHUM_COIL)) / (wOf(30, 80) - satW(DEHUM_COIL)), 0, DEHUM_MAX);
 
-// Intake filter (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 2): the fan's curve is a straight line from (0, fanMaxPa) through the
-// clean operating point (fanFlow, filterCleanPa); the filter's drop at fanFlow rises from clean to final with its loading,
-// in proportion to the flow. Where the two lines meet gives the intake's share of fanFlow. The filter loads with the air it
-// passes: filterLifeDays of fanFlow takes it to its change pressure. A room without these settings has no filter.
-export const FILTER_DEFAULTS = { fanMaxPa: 200, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 150, filterLifeDays: 30 };
+// Intake filter (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 2): the pressure a sensor across the filter reads while the intake runs
+// rises from clean to the change pressure with the loading (at most the fan's maximum pressure). The fan's curve is a
+// straight line from (0, fanMaxPa) through the clean operating point (fanFlow, filterCleanPa): at that pressure it moves
+// this share of fanFlow. The filter loads with the air it passes: filterLifeDays of fanFlow takes it to its change pressure.
+// A room without these settings has no filter.
+export const FILTER_DEFAULTS = { fanMaxPa: 200, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 80, filterLifeDays: 30 };
 export const hasFilter = (p) => [p.fanMaxPa, p.filterCleanPa, p.filterFinalPa].every(Number.isFinite) && p.filterLifeDays > 0;
 
 export function filterOf(params, Lf) {
-  if (!hasFilter(params)) return { share: 1, resistancePa: 0 };
+  if (!hasFilter(params)) return { share: 1, pressurePa: 0 };
   const { fanMaxPa, filterCleanPa, filterFinalPa } = params;
-  const resistancePa = filterCleanPa + (filterFinalPa - filterCleanPa) * Lf;
-  return { share: fanMaxPa / (fanMaxPa - filterCleanPa + resistancePa), resistancePa };
+  const pressurePa = Math.min(fanMaxPa, filterCleanPa + (filterFinalPa - filterCleanPa) * Lf);
+  const span = fanMaxPa - filterCleanPa;
+  return { share: span > 0 ? Math.max(0, (fanMaxPa - pressurePa) / span) : 1, pressurePa };
 }
 
 export const changeFilter = (state) => ({ ...state, Lf: 0 });
@@ -129,11 +131,11 @@ function exchangeOf(inputs, params, share = 1) {
 
 // The filter now: its pressure while the intake runs, the air through it, and the air the exhaust pulls in through the leaks.
 function filterNow(inputs, params, Lf) {
-  const { share, resistancePa } = filterOf(params, Lf);
+  const { share, pressurePa } = filterOf(params, Lf);
   const flow = params.fanFlow;
   return {
     share,
-    filterPa: inputs.intake ? resistancePa * share : 0,
+    filterPa: inputs.intake ? pressurePa : 0,
     throughM3h: inputs.intake ? (inputs.exhaust ? flow : SINGLE_FAN * flow) * share : 0,
     unfilteredM3h: inputs.intake && inputs.exhaust ? (flow * (1 - share)) / 2 : 0,
   };
```

Change `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md`:

```diff
diff --git a/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md b/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md
index f9d2c2a..1467a2c 100644
--- a/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md
+++ b/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md
@@ -19,18 +19,18 @@ Why: nothing in v2 knows about the filter on a room's intake. The simulator move
 | Item | Rule |
 |---|---|
 | Parameters | `fanFlow` (exists; now "with a clean filter"), `fanMaxPa`, `filterCleanPa`, `filterFinalPa` (section 3), `filterLifeDays` (section 8) |
-| State | the model state gains `Lf`, the filter's loading (0 = clean, 1 = at its final pressure, may go past 1). A state from before this iteration starts at `Lf` = 0 |
+| State | the model state gains `Lf`, the filter's loading (0 = clean, 1 = at its change pressure, may go past 1). A state from before this iteration starts at `Lf` = 0 |
 | Fan curve | a straight line from (0, `fanMaxPa`) through the clean operating point (`fanFlow`, `filterCleanPa`). The duct and grille are part of that clean point |
-| Filter resistance | `R` = `filterCleanPa` + (`filterFinalPa` − `filterCleanPa`) × `Lf`: the filter's pressure drop at `fanFlow`, in proportion to the flow at other flows |
-| Intake share | `f` = `fanMaxPa` ÷ (`fanMaxPa` − `filterCleanPa` + `R`): the intake flow ÷ `fanFlow` where the two lines meet (1 when clean; 0.65 at the final pressure with the defaults) |
-| Filter pressure | `R` × `f` while the intake fan runs, else 0: the drop at the flow the fan still moves, which is what a sensor reads. It is below `R`, so a sensor reads `filterFinalPa` only past `Lf` = 1 (at `Lf` = 4 with the defaults, `f` ≈ 0.31) |
+| Filter pressure | `p` = min(`fanMaxPa`, `filterCleanPa` + (`filterFinalPa` − `filterCleanPa`) × `Lf`): what a sensor across the filter reads while the intake runs (`filterFinalPa` at `Lf` = 1). `acting.filterPa` = `p` while the intake fan runs, else 0 |
+| Intake share | `f` = max(0, (`fanMaxPa` − `p`) ÷ (`fanMaxPa` − `filterCleanPa`)): the intake flow ÷ `fanFlow` on the fan's curve at `p` (1 when clean; 0.75 at the change pressure with the defaults; 0 at the fan's maximum) |
+| `filterOf(params, Lf)` | → `{ share, pressurePa }`; no filter: `{ share: 1, pressurePa: 0 }` |
 | Air exchange | both fans: `fanFlow` × (1 + `f`) ÷ 2 (the exhaust fan keeps pulling; the shortfall comes in through the leaks, unfiltered); intake alone: `SINGLE_FAN` × `fanFlow` × `f`; exhaust alone: as now; never below the leakage, as now |
 | Unfiltered share | both fans: (1 − `f`) ÷ (1 + `f`) of the exchange; reported, not used by the physics |
 | Loading | each sub-step `Lf` += air through the filter (m³) ÷ (`filterLifeDays` × 24 × `fanFlow`). Air through the filter = `fanFlow` × `f` (both fans) or `SINGLE_FAN` × `fanFlow` × `f` (intake alone), × the step time. The filter loads with the air it passes, not with the days |
 | Change | `changeFilter(state)` → the state with `Lf` = 0 |
 | Reply | `acting` gains `filterPa`, `intakeShare` (`f`), `unfilteredM3h`, `filterLoad` (`Lf`) |
 
-Example (defaults, Container Alpha, 150 m³/h, both fans on all the time): clean 150 m³/h all through the filter; after 30 days `Lf` ≈ 0.8, `f` ≈ 0.70; at `Lf` = 1, about 124 m³/h of exchange, 97 through the filter and 27 through the leaks.
+Example (defaults 40 / 80 / 200 Pa, Container Alpha, 150 m³/h, both fans on all the time): clean 150 m³/h all through the filter; after 30 days `Lf` ≈ 0.88, `f` ≈ 0.78 (`f` = 1 − `Lf` ÷ 4 gives `Lf` = 4 (1 − e^(−t ÷ 120 days))); at `Lf` = 1 the sensor reads 80 Pa, `f` = 0.75: 131 m³/h of exchange, 112.5 through the filter and 18.75 through the leaks.
 
 ## 3. Room settings

-| `filterFinalPa` | Filter change pressure | 150 Pa | above `filterCleanPa`, at most 2000, whole | `filterDirty`, simulation runs (prefill) |
+| `filterFinalPa` | Filter change pressure: the pressure sensor's reading at which to change the filter (with the intake running) | 80 Pa | above `filterCleanPa`, below `fanMaxPa`, whole | `filterDirty`, simulation runs (prefill) |
```

```diff
     return { error: "Filter change pressure must be above the clean filter's pressure drop", field: 'filterFinalPa' };

+  // The sensor cannot read more than the fan pushes: a change pressure at or above it is never reached.
+  if (pa('filterFinalPa') >= pa('fanMaxPa')) {
+    return { error: "Filter change pressure must be below the fan's maximum pressure", field: 'filterFinalPa' };
+  }
   // The later field of a clashing pair is blamed.
   for (let i = 1; i < ADDRESSES.length; i++) {
     const { field } = ADDRESSES[i];
```

### Task D2: filterDirty and filterMissing count across exchanges

**Files:**
- Modify: `README.md`
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md`
- Modify: `server/alarms.js`
- Modify: `server/filter.js`
- Modify: `server/simulate.js`
- Modify: `tests/alarms-filter.test.js`
- Modify: `tests/filter.test.js`

`server/filter.js`: `countingFrom(log, now)` (when the intake's present run passed its 60 s spin-up, null while off or spinning up); `filterInput` gives `pa` only while the intake is past its spin-up and the sensor answered its last poll, and adds `countFrom`. `server/alarms.js`: the two pressure alarms add up counted time (each evaluation with a counted reading in the raising range adds the time since the room's previous evaluation, none from before `countFrom`); no reading or a reading in the hysteresis pauses the count, a reading in the release range, **Filter changed** (`reset`) or no sensor starts it again; `forget` drops the counts. `server/simulate.js` starts its count again only below the change pressure − 10, like the alarm. Spec section 5 (the counting rule, with the 3-min exchange example) and 8, and the README follow.

```diff
@@ -40,7 +40,7 @@ Machines work the same way: add a relay module and an aircon gateway on the devi
 
 **Dehumidifier.** A room can have a dehumidifier on output 5 of its relay module (Edit room → Power and water → "Dehumidifier fitted", with its power and its capacity in L/day as on its label). Auto switches it on at the upper humidity limit and off at the middle of the band, only once the humidifier has been off for 30 min (a room that needed the humidifier a moment ago is not wet by itself: an air exchange wets it for a few minutes) and never together with the humidifier; its compressor gets 3 min between changes. A fitted dehumidifier has a machine tile, a timeline row, alarms and a share of the energy; the Simulate stepper asks for its size (0 = none) and the results show the water it took out. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-21-dehumidifier-design.md`.
 
-**Intake filter.** Each room has a filter on its intake fan (Edit room → Intake filter: the fan's maximum pressure from its datasheet, the clean filter's pressure drop, the change pressure: the filter pressure sensor's reading at which to change it with the intake running, 80 Pa by default and below the fan's maximum, and how often to change it, 0 = no reminder). The room page's **Intake filter** card shows the days in use and the due date, and **Filter changed** records a new filter (the days start again, the filter alarms clear). With an optional filter pressure sensor (Edit room → Sensors → "Filter pressure sensor address") the card shows the pressure across the filter; readings count once the intake has run 60 s. Alarms: the filter due, the pressure at or above the change pressure for 10 min (dirty), and below half the clean drop for 10 min (missing or torn, or the sensor tube is off). With a sensor the intake fan's estimated energy rises with the pressure. In the simulator a filter loads with the air it passes and the fan moves less air as it does (the exhaust fan pulls the shortfall in through the leaks, unfiltered); the device app's sim card shows the filter and has a **Change filter** button (a Reset keeps the filter as it is). Simulation runs take the room's filter settings and its days in use, change it on the reminder (or at the change pressure with a sensor) and report the changes, the mean filter pressure and the unfiltered share. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md`.
+**Intake filter.** Each room has a filter on its intake fan (Edit room → Intake filter: the fan's maximum pressure from its datasheet, the clean filter's pressure drop, the change pressure: the filter pressure sensor's reading at which to change it with the intake running, 80 Pa by default and below the fan's maximum, and how often to change it, 0 = no reminder). The room page's **Intake filter** card shows the days in use and the due date, and **Filter changed** records a new filter (the days start again, the filter alarms clear). With an optional filter pressure sensor (Edit room → Sensors → "Filter pressure sensor address") the card shows the pressure across the filter; readings count once the intake has run 60 s. Alarms: the filter due, the pressure at or above the change pressure (dirty) and below half the clean drop (missing or torn, or the sensor tube is off), each after 10 min of intake running with such readings, added up across exchanges. With a sensor the intake fan's estimated energy rises with the pressure. In the simulator a filter loads with the air it passes and the fan moves less air as it does (the exhaust fan pulls the shortfall in through the leaks, unfiltered); the device app's sim card shows the filter and has a **Change filter** button (a Reset keeps the filter as it is). Simulation runs take the room's filter settings and its days in use, change it on the reminder (or at the change pressure with a sensor) and report the changes, the mean filter pressure and the unfiltered share. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md`.
 
 **Simulation runs.** On the Rooms list (or a room's Simulations card), **Simulate** asks in three steps for the room's walls, weather and machines (wall heat transfer, outdoor day high, night low and dew point, sun on the roof, fan flow, aircon size, humidifier output), the crops and the start stage, and how long (7 days, or the whole program). The run goes on in the background (a 68-day program takes a few seconds) with the room's own control settings, and its page shows the time in band (temperature, humidity, CO₂, all three; per stage), charts with the band as it moved and the outdoor air, the machine timeline, and the energy (kWh, RM, per machine and per day) and water (litres). The aircon energy comes from the size you enter (COP 3). The last 20 runs of each room are kept. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-20-crops-and-simulation-design.md`.
```

Change `server/filter.js`:

```diff
diff --git a/server/filter.js b/server/filter.js
index 4a77d83..93b9a76 100644
--- a/server/filter.js
+++ b/server/filter.js
@@ -17,6 +17,18 @@ export function intakeOnThrough(log, from, to) {
   return log.every((r) => r.at <= from || r.at > to || r.on);
 }
 
+// When the intake's present run passed its spin-up (COUNT_AFTER_MS after it was switched on), or null while it is off or
+// still spinning up. log: as for intakeOnThrough, oldest first.
+export function countingFrom(log, now) {
+  let since = null;
+  for (const r of log) {
+    if (r.at > now) break;
+    if (!r.on) since = null;
+    else if (since === null) since = r.at;
+  }
+  return since !== null && now - since >= COUNT_AFTER_MS ? since + COUNT_AFTER_MS : null;
+}
+
 // The newest good reading [{ at, pa, ok? }] (oldest first) at most maxAgeMs old and taken after the intake had been on
 // COUNT_AFTER_MS without a break: { at, pa }, or null. With the fan off the sensor reads about 0 and says nothing.
 export function countedPa(readings, intakeLog, now, maxAgeMs = MAX_AGE_MS) {
@@ -40,14 +52,21 @@ export function daysInUse(room, now) {
   return room.filterChangedAt === null ? 0 : Math.max(0, (now - room.filterChangedAt) / DAY_MS);
 }
 
-// The `filter` input of alarms.evaluate: { sensor, pa (the counted pressure or null), cleanPa, finalPa, changeDays, daysInUse }.
+// The `filter` input of alarms.evaluate: { sensor, pa, countFrom, cleanPa, finalPa, changeDays, daysInUse }. pa: the counted
+// pressure now, null unless the intake is past its spin-up now and the sensor answered its last poll (the alarms' count
+// pauses). countFrom: when the intake's present run passed its spin-up (at most this far back: the log window), or null.
 export function filterInput(db, room, now) {
   const sensor = room.dpAddress !== null;
-  const counted = sensor
-    ? countedPa(db.filterReadingsWindow(room.id, now - MAX_AGE_MS, now + 1), db.machineLogWindow(room.id, 'intake', now - MAX_AGE_MS - COUNT_AFTER_MS, now), now)
-    : null;
+  let pa = null, countFrom = null;
+  if (sensor) {
+    const log = db.machineLogWindow(room.id, 'intake', now - MAX_AGE_MS - COUNT_AFTER_MS, now);
+    countFrom = countingFrom(log, now);
+    if (countFrom !== null && db.getFilterSensor(room.id, 1)?.status === 'online') {
+      pa = countedPa(db.filterReadingsWindow(room.id, now - MAX_AGE_MS, now + 1), log, now)?.pa ?? null;
+    }
+  }
   return {
-    sensor, pa: counted?.pa ?? null, cleanPa: room.filterCleanPa, finalPa: room.filterFinalPa,
+    sensor, pa, countFrom, cleanPa: room.filterCleanPa, finalPa: room.filterFinalPa,
     changeDays: room.filterChangeDays, daysInUse: daysInUse(room, now),
   };
 }
```
