### Browser check (after D2)

As in part C, with throwaway apps only (other ports, temp files), stopped by their PIDs afterwards. A room made without filter
settings, the device `dp` sensor at 85.3 Pa, and a 14-day run with `filterLifeDays` 5, `filterUsedDays` 3, `filterChangeDays` 0,
`filterSensor` true. What was seen:

| Page | Seen |
|---|---|
| New room | defaults 200 / 40 / 80 Pa, every 30 days; a save with change pressure 200 is a 400 "Filter change pressure must be below the fan's maximum pressure" (field `filterFinalPa`) |
| Room page | **Intake filter** card: 85.3 Pa, "clean 40 Pa · change at 80 Pa · Online", "At or above the change pressure: change the filter" |
| Edit room | change pressure 200: the error under the field, and the help line "Filter change pressure: the pressure sensor's reading at which to change the filter (with the intake running)…" |
| Run page | input line "… filter 40 Pa clean, change at 80 Pa, 3 days in use, changed at the change pressure"; **Intake filter** card: 1 change (day 6 00:56), 62.3 Pa, 7.5 %, 47 m³/h; the mark on the intake row |


## Part E: review fixes

The fixes of three reviews of ee4d30a..0894bba. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md`. Base: parts A to D
applied (the suite: 1183 tests, all passing); after E3: 1197, all passing. Every block below is generated from the commits that
were run (branch `worktree-agent-a58413333a00ba2d8`, d7d963b, 25b205b and 837d37d).

Conventions for every task:

- Run tests with `node --disable-warning=ExperimentalWarning --test <files>`; the whole suite with `npm test`.
- New files are given whole. Changes to existing files are unified diffs against the file as the previous task left it; apply
  them by hand or with `git apply` (save the block to a file first).
- Commit messages follow the repo (a plain sentence, no prefix, no trailers).
- Never start, stop or restart the owner's apps (`npm start`, `npm run device`), never open `data/*.db`. A browser check uses a
  throwaway device and farm app on other ports with temp files (see "Browser check" at the end).

### Task E1: Review fixes, server

**Files:**
- Modify: `device/sim-power.js`
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md`
- Modify: `server/api.js`
- Modify: `server/controller.js`
- Modify: `server/filter.js`
- Create: `tests/filter-review-server.test.js`

S1: `server/controller.js` reads the room again (`db.getRoom(room.id) ?? room`) before `filterInput`, so a **Filter changed** that lands while an earlier room of the same run is sending is not undone by the stale room. S2: `server/api.js` merges the room's stored four filter settings into a PUT body before `validateRoom`, so the cross checks compare what will be saved. S3: `filterInput(db, room, now, view)` counts a reading only while the machines view, when it has the relay module, says the intake is `on` (the controller passes its view). S4: `fanWattFactor` takes the pressure at most `fanMaxPa` (never above 1.15). S5: readings from before `filterChangedAt` never count, for the alarms or as `pa_before`. S6: `meanCountedPa` in one forward pass. Spec sections 3, 4, 5 and 6 follow.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tests/filter-review-server.test.js`:

```js
// Review fixes of the filter on the farm server (spec 2026-10-22-filter-design.md 3 to 6): a filter change during another
// room's sends, a partial room save against the stored settings, the intake really running, the fan factor's ceiling,
// readings of the old filter, and the daily mean in one pass.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { openDb } from '../server/db.js';
import { createApp } from '../server/app.js';
import { createController } from '../server/controller.js';
import { changeFilter, filterInput, meanCountedPa, intakeOnThrough } from '../server/filter.js';
import { fanWattFactor } from '../device/sim-power.js';

const S = 1000;
const MIN = 60_000;
const DAY = 86_400_000;
const ROOM = { name: 'Room 1', length: 6, width: 4, height: 2.5 };

test('a Filter changed during another room\'s sends is not undone: the room is read again before its filter alarms', async (t) => {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  t.after(() => db.close());
  const a = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, name: 'A', relayAddress: 3 }, 0).id;
  const b = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, name: 'B', filterChangeDays: 1 }, 0).id;
  let release;
  const held = new Promise((r) => { release = r; });
  let sends = 0;
  const machines = { get: () => null, async command() { if (sends++ === 0) await held; }, forget() {} };
  const clock = 2 * DAY;
  const controller = createController({ db, machines, now: () => clock });
  controller.update(a, { auto: true });
  const running = controller.run(clock); // room A sends its first command and waits there
  await new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r));
  assert.equal(sends, 1, 'room A is sending');
  changeFilter(db, b, clock); // the API's Filter changed for room B
  controller.filterChanged(b);
  release();
  await running;
  assert.deepEqual(db.activeAlarms(b).map((x) => x.key), [], 'room B: just changed, not due');
});

async function app(t) {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  const server = createApp(db, {});
  await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
  t.after(async () => { await new Promise((r) => server.close(r)); db.close(); });
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  const call = async (method, path, body) => {
    const res = await fetch(base + path, { method, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
    return { status: res.status, body: await res.json() };
  };
  return { db, call };
}

test('a save with some filter settings is checked against the room\'s stored others, not the defaults', async (t) => {
  const { call } = await app(t);
  const big = (await call('POST', '/api/rooms', { ...ROOM, fanMaxPa: 500, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 300 })).body;
  const ok = await call('PUT', `/api/rooms/${big.id}`, { ...ROOM, name: 'Big', filterFinalPa: 300 });
  assert.equal(ok.status, 200, 'fan maximum 500 is stored: 300 is below it');
  assert.deepEqual([ok.body.fanMaxPa, ok.body.filterFinalPa], [500, 300]);
  const small = (await call('POST', '/api/rooms', { ...ROOM, name: 'Small', fanMaxPa: 100, filterCleanPa: 40, filterFinalPa: 80 })).body;
  const bad = await call('PUT', `/api/rooms/${small.id}`, { ...ROOM, name: 'Small', filterFinalPa: 150 });
  assert.deepEqual(bad, { status: 400, body: { error: "Filter change pressure must be below the fan's maximum pressure", field: 'filterFinalPa' } });
});

function room(t, fields = {}) {
  const db = openDb(':memory:');
  t.after(() => db.close());
  const r = db.createRoom({ ...ROOM, relayAddress: 3, dpAddress: 7, ...fields }, 0);
  return { db, id: r.id };
}
const view = (status) => ({ relay: { machines: [{ key: 'intake', status }] } });

test('a reading counts only while the intake really runs when the machines view says so; without a view, as commanded', (t) => {
  const { db, id } = room(t);
  db.logMachine(id, 'intake', { on: true }, 'auto', DAY);
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: DAY + 5 * MIN, ok: true, pa: 12 });
  const at = DAY + 5 * MIN + 2 * S;
  assert.equal(filterInput(db, db.getRoom(id), at).pa, 12, 'no view: the commanded rule');
  assert.equal(filterInput(db, db.getRoom(id), at, view('on')).pa, 12);
  for (const status of ['not-running', 'no-answer', 'switching', 'not-following']) {
    assert.equal(filterInput(db, db.getRoom(id), at, view(status)).pa, null, status);
  }
  assert.equal(filterInput(db, db.getRoom(id), at, view('not-wired')).pa, 12, 'no relay: the commanded rule');
});

test('the controller passes the machines view: a dead intake fan does not raise filterMissing', async (t) => {
  const { db, id } = room(t);
  db.logMachine(id, 'intake', { on: true }, 'manual', 0);
  const machines = { get: () => view('not-running'), async command() {}, forget() {} };
  let clock = 0;
  const controller = createController({ db, machines, now: () => clock });
  for (let i = 1; i <= 15; i++) {
    clock = i * MIN;
    db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: clock, ok: true, pa: 1 }); // the fan stands still: about 0 Pa
    await controller.run(clock);
  }
  assert.equal(db.activeAlarms(id).some((x) => x.key === 'filterMissing'), false);
});

test('the fan factor never exceeds 1.15, even for a mean far above the fan maximum', () => {
  assert.equal(fanWattFactor(2000, 40, 200), 1.15);
  assert.equal(fanWattFactor(201, 40, 200), 1.15);
});

test('readings of the old filter do not count: not for the alarms, not as the pressure before the next change', (t) => {
  const { db, id } = room(t);
  db.logMachine(id, 'intake', { on: true }, 'auto', 0);
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: DAY, ok: true, pa: 160 });
  changeFilter(db, id, DAY + 10 * S); // the fan kept running through the change
  assert.equal(filterInput(db, db.getRoom(id), DAY + 20 * S).pa, null, 'the 160 Pa was the old filter');
  db.addFilterReading({ roomId: id, address: 7, at: DAY + 30 * S, ok: true, pa: 41 });
  assert.equal(filterInput(db, db.getRoom(id), DAY + 35 * S).pa, 41);
  changeFilter(db, id, DAY + 5 * MIN);
  assert.deepEqual(db.filterChanges(id).map((c) => c.paBefore), [41, 160]);
  changeFilter(db, id, DAY + 6 * MIN);
  assert.equal(db.filterChanges(id)[0].paBefore, null, 'no reading since the last change');
});

// The day mean as it was: every reading against the whole log.
const oldMean = (readings, log) => {
  const counted = readings.filter((r) => intakeOnThrough(log, r.at - MIN, r.at));
  return counted.length === 0 ? null : counted.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.pa, 0) / counted.length;
};

test('meanCountedPa in one pass gives what the reading-by-reading rule gave, on a mixed day', () => {
  const log = [];
  let on = false;
  for (let at = 0; at < DAY; at += 7 * MIN + 13 * S) {
    on = !on;
    log.push({ at, on });
    if (at % 3 === 0) log.push({ at, on }); // a repeated row
  }
  const readings = [];
  for (let at = 0; at < DAY; at += 37 * S) readings.push({ at, pa: 30 + ((at / S) % 50) });
  assert.equal(meanCountedPa(readings, log), oldMean(readings, log));
  assert.equal(meanCountedPa(readings.slice(0, 3), [{ at: 0, on: true }]), oldMean(readings.slice(0, 3), [{ at: 0, on: true }]));
  assert.equal(meanCountedPa([], log), null);
  assert.equal(meanCountedPa(readings, []), null);
  // an off and an on at the same time, and the edges of the spin-up
  const edge = [{ at: 0, on: true }, { at: 100 * S, on: false }, { at: 100 * S, on: true }];
  const r = [60 * S, 99 * S, 100 * S, 159 * S, 160 * S, 200 * S].map((at) => ({ at, pa: at / S }));
  assert.equal(meanCountedPa(r, edge), oldMean(r, edge));
});
```

 ## 4. Pressure sensor
 
@@ -62,14 +62,14 @@ The room dialog gets a section **Intake filter** with the first four fields; `dp
 | Bad reading | outside −50 to 2000 Pa: stored with `ok` 0, the value kept, and the error "Out of range" |
 | Status | as the energy meter: online, timeout (1–2 failed in a row), offline (3 or more), `no-data`, `not-wired` (no address) |
 | Storage | table `filter_readings (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, room_id INTEGER NOT NULL, address INTEGER NOT NULL, at INTEGER NOT NULL, ok INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (ok IN (0,1)), pa REAL, error TEXT)`, the energy meter readings' three indexes on `(room_id, address, …)`; deleted with the room |
-| Counts when | the intake fan has been commanded on for at least 60 s (from `machine_log`); a reading at other times is stored and shown but judged by nothing |
+| Counts when | the intake fan has been commanded on for at least 60 s (from `machine_log`) and, when the controller has a machines view with the relay module, its intake status is `on` (a dead fan or a relay that does not answer reads about 0 Pa and must not look like a missing filter); a reading from before the last filter change is the old filter's and never counts. A reading at other times is stored and shown but judged by nothing. The controller reads the room again before it judges the filter (a **Filter changed** may land while it sends to another room) |
 | API | `GET /api/rooms/:id/sensors` returns a fourth element `{ kind: 'dp', address, status, failuresInRow, lastOk: { at, pa } \| null, readings }`, like the energy meter |
 
 ## 5. Change reminder and alarms
 
 | Item | Rule |
 |---|---|
-| **Filter changed** | `POST /api/rooms/:id/filter/changed` → sets `filterChangedAt` to now, adds a row to `filter_changes (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, room_id INTEGER NOT NULL, at INTEGER NOT NULL, pa_before REAL)` (`pa_before`: the last counted reading of the past day, or null: the intake is usually off while the filter is changed), clears `filterDue`, `filterDirty` and `filterMissing`; returns the room. `GET /api/rooms/:id/filter/changes` → the last 20, newest first |
+| **Filter changed** | `POST /api/rooms/:id/filter/changed` → sets `filterChangedAt` to now, adds a row to `filter_changes (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, room_id INTEGER NOT NULL, at INTEGER NOT NULL, pa_before REAL)` (`pa_before`: the last counted reading of the past day since the previous change, or null: the intake is usually off while the filter is changed), clears `filterDue`, `filterDirty` and `filterMissing`; returns the room. `GET /api/rooms/:id/filter/changes` → the last 20, newest first |
 | `filterDue` (warning, no delay) | `filterChangeDays` > 0 and days since `filterChangedAt` ≥ `filterChangeDays`. Text "Filter in use 32 days, change every 30". Clears on **Filter changed** or when the setting moves the due date past now |
 | `filterDirty` (warning, 10 min counted) | a sensor is set and the counted ΔP ≥ `filterFinalPa`. Text "Filter pressure 85 Pa, at or above the change pressure 80". Clears below `filterFinalPa` − 10 or on **Filter changed** |
 | `filterMissing` (warning, 10 min counted) | a sensor is set and the counted ΔP < `filterCleanPa` ÷ 2. Text "Filter pressure 12 Pa: filter missing or torn, or the sensor tube is off". Clears at or above 0.6 × `filterCleanPa` or on **Filter changed** |
@@ -80,7 +80,7 @@ The room dialog gets a section **Intake filter** with the first four fields; `dp
 
 | Item | Rule |
 |---|---|
-| Intake fan watts | rated × (1 + 0.15 × (p − `filterCleanPa`) ÷ (`fanMaxPa` − `filterCleanPa`)), never below rated: rated with a clean filter, 1.15 × rated at the fan's maximum pressure. Small fixed-speed AC fans lose air, not watts, on a dirty filter |
+| Intake fan watts | rated × (1 + 0.15 × (p − `filterCleanPa`) ÷ (`fanMaxPa` − `filterCleanPa`)), never below rated and never above 1.15 × rated (p is taken at most `fanMaxPa`): rated with a clean filter, 1.15 × rated at the fan's maximum pressure. Small fixed-speed AC fans lose air, not watts, on a dirty filter |
 | Simulator (`device/sim-power.js`, `server/simulate.js`) | p = the model's `filterPa` |
 | Real-room estimate (`server/usage.js`) | with a sensor: p = the day's mean counted ΔP (no counted reading that day: rated); without a sensor: rated, as now |
 | Exhaust and circulation fans | unchanged |

### Task E2: Review fixes, physics

**Files:**
- Modify: `device/sim-model.js`
- Modify: `device/sim-store.js`
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-filter-design.md`
- Modify: `server/simulate.js`

P1: `full()` in `device/sim-model.js` and `airState` in `device/sim-store.js` keep a loading only when finite and never below 0. P2: `filterOf` takes a missing loading as 0. P3: `hasFilter` without an array, computed once per `step()` and passed to `substep`; the filter's share and pressure are plain numbers per sub-step (no object). A 68-day run gives the same numbers as before (compared byte for byte without and with a filter) and takes the same time within noise: about 2.8–2.9 s without a filter, before the filter existed and now, 3.0–3.2 s with one. A test pins a 3-day run without a filter to the numbers of v2 ee4d30a. P4: the comment on the unfiltered share. P5: spec section 10 (0.75) and section 8 (the required run fields).

+    energy: { byMachine: { intake: 1.19, exhaust: 1.19, circulation: 4.32, humidifier: 0.66, dehumidifier: 0, ac: 17.24 }, kWh: 24.6, cost: 13.53 },
+    water: { litres: 16.5, removedLitres: 0, cost: 0 },
+    extremes: { tMin: 25, tMax: 25.2, rhMin: 84.8, rhMax: 91.2, co2Max: 903 },
+    series: 864,
+    last: { s: 258900, t: 25.07, rh: 87.2, co2: 859, outT: 26.2, outRH: 88.2, tLow: 24, tHigh: 26, rhLow: 85, rhHigh: 91, co2Low: 700, co2High: 900, stage: 'fruiting' },
+  });
+  assert.equal(r.summary.filter, null);
+});
```

 | Item | Rule |
 |---|---|
-| New physics fields | `fanMaxPa`, `filterCleanPa`, `filterFinalPa`, `filterChangeDays` (prefilled from the room), `filterLifeDays` (default 30, 1–365), `filterUsedDays` (the filter's age at the start: prefilled with the room's whole days since `filterChangedAt`, 0–365), `filterSensor` (true when the room has `dpAddress`; not asked). As the other fields, the stepper prefills from the room's last run first, except `filterUsedDays`, always the room's now. Any filter field sent: the first four are required; `filterChangeDays` and `filterUsedDays` default to 0, `filterSensor` to false. None sent: no filter |
+| New physics fields | `fanMaxPa`, `filterCleanPa`, `filterFinalPa`, `filterChangeDays` (prefilled from the room), `filterLifeDays` (default 30, 1–365), `filterUsedDays` (the filter's age at the start: prefilled with the room's whole days since `filterChangedAt`, 0–365), `filterSensor` (true when the room has `dpAddress`; not asked). As the other fields, the stepper prefills from the room's last run first, except `filterUsedDays`, always the room's now. When any of the seven is sent, `fanMaxPa`, `filterCleanPa`, `filterFinalPa` and `filterLifeDays` are required (whole numbers in the device app's ranges, with the three checks of section 3); `filterChangeDays` and `filterUsedDays` default to 0 and `filterSensor` to false. None of the seven sent: no filter |
 | Start | `Lf` = `filterUsedDays` ÷ `filterLifeDays`, as if the filter had passed `fanFlow` all that time (a conservative start) |
 | Changes in the run | at the start of a 10 s step, the filter is changed (`Lf` = 0) when `filterChangeDays` > 0 and that many days passed since the last change (before the run: `filterUsedDays` ago, so a filter in use that long is changed at once), or, with `filterSensor`, after 10 min of intake running at or above `filterFinalPa`, added up over the exchanges like the `filterDirty` alarm (it starts again only below `filterFinalPa` − 10; the model has no spin-up) |
-| Results | `summary.filter` = `{ changes (s from the start), meanExchangeM3h (over the whole run), meanFilterPa (while the intake ran; null if it never ran), unfilteredPct (of the exchange while the intake ran, the part through the leaks) }`, or null without a filter. The page shows them as an **Intake filter** card, the changes as marks on the intake row and the filter in the input line |
+| Results | `summary.filter` = `{ changes (s from the start), meanExchangeM3h (over the whole run), meanFilterPa (while the intake ran; null if it never ran), unfilteredPct (of all the air that came in while the intake ran, with the exhaust or alone, the part through the leaks) }`, or null without a filter. The page shows them as an **Intake filter** card, the changes as marks on the intake row and the filter in the input line |
 | Old runs | runs saved before this iteration show no filter lines; a run without filter fields has no filter (`Lf` 0) |
 | Suggest machines | unchanged: it passes no filter fields, and a run without them keeps `Lf` at 0 (`f` = 1) |
 
@@ -116,7 +116,7 @@ The room dialog gets a section **Intake filter** with the first four fields; `dp
 
 | Area | Cases |
 |---|---|
-| Physics | `f` = 1 clean; 0.65 at `Lf` 1 with the defaults; falls past 1; the pair, intake-alone, exhaust-alone and no-fan exchanges; loading in proportion to the air through the filter (half the run time, half the loading); `changeFilter`; a state without `Lf` |
+| Physics | `f` = 1 clean; 0.75 at `Lf` 1 with the defaults; falls past 1; the pair, intake-alone, exhaust-alone and no-fan exchanges; loading in proportion to the air through the filter (half the run time, half the loading); `changeFilter`; a state without `Lf` |
 | Power | rated at clean, 1.15 × rated at `fanMaxPa`, never below rated |
 | Room settings | defaults, ranges, the two cross checks, `dpAddress` uniqueness against every other address and the outdoor sensor |
 | Sensor | register decode (signed × 10), out of range, statuses, polling order, counted only after 60 s of intake |
```

+  assert.deepEqual(filterBar(room, 19.9), { fill: 19.9 / 200, cleanAt: 0.2, finalAt: 0.4, tone: 'warn' }, 'below half the clean drop: missing or torn');
+  assert.equal(filterBar(room, 20).tone, 'ok');
+  assert.equal(filterBar(room, 79.9).tone, 'ok');
+  assert.equal(filterBar(room, 80).tone, 'bad');
+  assert.deepEqual(filterBar(room, 250), { fill: 1, cleanAt: 0.2, finalAt: 0.4, tone: 'bad' }, 'above the fan maximum: full');
+  assert.equal(filterBar(room, -5).fill, 0);

+  assert.equal(changesStale({ seenChangedAt: 100, failed: false }, { filterChangedAt: 200 }), true, 'a change made elsewhere');
+  assert.equal(changesStale({ seenChangedAt: 100, failed: true }, { filterChangedAt: 100 }), true);
+  assert.equal(changesStale({ seenChangedAt: undefined, failed: false }, { filterChangedAt: 100 }), true, 'never loaded');
+  const now = new Date(2026, 9, 22, 12).getTime();

+  assert.equal(filterCard({ room, sensor: null, changes: [], now }).changesError, null);
 });

As the other fields, the stepper prefills from the room's last run first, except `filterUsedDays`, always the room's now. When any of the seven is sent, `fanMaxPa`, `filterCleanPa`, `filterFinalPa` and `filterLifeDays` are required (whole numbers in the device app's ranges, with the three checks of section 3); `filterChangeDays` and `filterUsedDays` default to 0 and `filterSensor` to false. None of the seven sent: no filter |
 | Start | `Lf` = `filterUsedDays` ÷ `filterLifeDays`, as if the filter had passed `fanFlow` all that time (a conservative start) |
 | Changes in the run | at the start of a 10 s step, the filter is changed (`Lf` = 0) when `filterChangeDays` > 0 and that many days passed since the last change (before the run: `filterUsedDays` ago, so a filter in use that long is changed at once), or, with `filterSensor`, after 10 min of intake running at or above `filterFinalPa`, added up over the exchanges like the `filterDirty` alarm (it starts again only below `filterFinalPa` − 10; the model has no spin-up) |
-| Results | `summary.filter` = `{ changes (s from the start), meanExchangeM3h (over the whole run), meanFilterPa (while the intake ran; null if it never ran), unfilteredPct (of all the air that came in while the intake ran, with the exhaust or alone, the part through the leaks) }`, or null without a filter. +| Results | `summary.filter` = `{ changes (s from the start), meanExchangeM3h (over the whole run), meanFilterPa (while the intake ran; null if it never ran), unfilteredPct (of all the air that came in while the intake ran, with the exhaust or alone, the part through the leaks) }`, or null without a filter. The page shows them as an **Intake filter** card, the changes as marks "Filter" on the intake row and the filter in the input line |
 | Old runs | runs saved before this iteration show no filter lines; a run without filter fields has no filter (`Lf` 0) |
 | Suggest machines | unchanged: it passes no filter fields, and a run without them keeps `Lf` at 0 (`f` = 1) |

Change `web/filter-math.js`:

```diff
diff --git a/web/filter-math.js b/web/filter-math.js
index 33267fd..ad833d6 100644
--- a/web/filter-math.js
+++ b/web/filter-math.js
@@ -47,10 +47,33 @@ export function parseFilter(texts) {
 // The filter pressure sensor address: blank = none, else a whole number 1 to 247; NaN if wrong.
 export const dpAddressOf = meterAddressOf;
 
+const days = (n) => `${n} ${n === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'}`;
+// Whole local calendar days from `from` to `to` (0: the same day).
+const calendarDays = (from, to) => {
+  const a = new Date(from), b = new Date(to);
+  return Math.round((new Date(b.getFullYear(), b.getMonth(), b.getDate()) - new Date(a.getFullYear(), a.getMonth(), a.getDate())) / DAY_MS);
+};
+const clamp01 = (x) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, x));
+
+// The bar of the Filter card: the reading (null: none yet) over a scale from 0 to the fan's maximum pressure. fill, cleanAt
+// and finalAt are shares of the scale; tone: 'warn' below half the clean drop (the missing range), 'ok' up to the change
+// pressure, 'bad' at or above it, null without a reading.
+export function filterBar(room, value) {
+  const max = room.fanMaxPa;
+  const at = (pa) => clamp01(pa / max);
+  const tone = value === null ? null : value < room.filterCleanPa / 2 ? 'warn' : value >= room.filterFinalPa ? 'bad' : 'ok';
+  return { fill: value === null ? 0 : at(value), cleanAt: at(room.filterCleanPa), finalAt: at(room.filterFinalPa), tone };
+}
+
+// Whether the card must ask for the changes again: the room's change time is not the one last loaded (a change made on another
+// page), or the last load failed. seen: { seenChangedAt (undefined: never loaded), failed }.
+export const changesStale = (seen, room) => seen.failed || seen.seenChangedAt !== room.filterChangedAt;
+
 // The Filter card of the room page. room: GET /api/rooms/:id; sensor: the 'dp' element of the sensors reply (or null);
-// changes: GET /api/rooms/:id/filter/changes. →
-// { pressure: { text, status, value, min, clean, final, max, over } | null (no sensor), noSensor, age, due, overdue, changes: [text] }.
-export function filterCard({ room, sensor, changes, now }) {
+// changes: GET /api/rooms/:id/filter/changes; changesFailed: that list could not be loaded. →
+// { pressure: { text, status, value, min, clean, final, max, over, bar } | null (no sensor), noSensor, age, due, overdue,
+//   changes: [text], changesError (text or null) }.
+export function filterCard({ room, sensor, changes, now, changesFailed = false }) {
   const hasSensor = finite(room?.dpAddress) && sensor && sensor.status !== 'not-wired';
   let pressure = null;
   if (hasSensor) {
@@ -60,20 +83,22 @@ export function filterCard({ room, sensor, changes, now }) {
       status: STATUS_WORDS[sensor.status] ?? String(sensor.status),
       value: pa, min: 0, clean: room.filterCleanPa, final: room.filterFinalPa, max: Math.max(room.fanMaxPa, pa ?? 0),
       over: pa !== null && pa >= room.filterFinalPa,
+      bar: filterBar(room, pa),
     };
   }
   const since = finite(room?.filterChangedAt) ? room.filterChangedAt : now;
-  const days = Math.max(0, Math.floor((now - since) / DAY_MS));
+  const inUse = Math.max(0, calendarDays(since, now));
   const every = room?.filterChangeDays ?? 0;
   const dueAt = since + every * DAY_MS;
   const overdue = every > 0 && now >= dueAt;
   return {
     pressure,
     noSensor: 'No pressure sensor',
-    age: (now - since) < DAY_MS ? 'Changed today' : `${days} ${days === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'} in use`,
-    due: every > 0 ? `${overdue ? 'Due since' : 'Change by'} ${dayMonth(dueAt)} (every ${every} days)` : 'No reminder',
+    age: inUse === 0 ? 'Changed today' : `${days(inUse)} in use`,
+    due: every > 0 ? `${overdue ? 'Due since' : 'Change by'} ${dayMonth(dueAt)} (every ${days(every)})` : 'No reminder',
     overdue,
     changes: (Array.isArray(changes) ? changes : []).slice(0, 5)
       .map((c) => `Changed ${dateTime(c.at)}${finite(c.paBefore) ? ` at ${Math.round(c.paBefore)} Pa` : ''}`),
+    changesError: changesFailed ? "Couldn't load the filter changes" : null,
   };
 }

### Browser check (after E3)

Throwaway apps only (other ports, temp files), stopped by their PIDs afterwards. Three rooms with only a `dp` sensor, at 62.4,
12 and 95 Pa (defaults 200 / 40 / 80 Pa), and a 14-day run changing the filter every day. What was seen:

| Page | Seen |
|---|---|
| Room at 62.4 Pa | green fill to 31 %, ticks at 20 % (clean) and 40 % (change), "Change by 24 Oct (every 1 day)" |
| Room at 12 Pa | amber fill (below half the clean drop) |
| Room at 95 Pa | red fill past the change tick and "At or above the change pressure: change the filter" |
| Light mode | the same bar and ticks; the amber is the theme's darker warning tone |
| Run page | 13 "Filter" marks on the intake row, about 57 px apart, none overlapping |
